# Wilson's Engineering Plans
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Blaze Slayer
[Wilson's Engineering Plans]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Inferno Demonlord]] drop
Uses: [[SOS Flare]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WILSON_ENGINEERING_PLANS
Tooltip: Drops very rarely from the Inferno Demonlord.; Right-click to view recipes!; LEGENDARY
Wilson's Engineering Plans are a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] drop from the Tier IV [[Inferno Demonlord]]. The drop chance is 0.09% and is in the [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] category. It is used in the recipe for [[SOS Flare]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[SOS Flare]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Wilson's Engineering Plans]] 1x Wilson's Engineering Plans 1x [[Inferno Apex]] 1x [[Alert Flare]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 32x [[Enchanted Sulphur]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x32 + [[Inferno Apex]] + [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + Wilson's Engineering Plans + [[Alert Flare]] → [[SOS Flare]]|
## Trivia
- Wilson's Engineering Plans is a reference to Edward Wilson Very, the inventor of the Flare Gun.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 21, 2022|Added Wilson's Engineering Plans.|

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Wilson's Engineering Plans.|

A Winter Water Orb can be given to [[Terry]] in exchange for his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.

Wise Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] Set that is focused on [[Mana]] and abilities. The full set can be crafted from 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Dragon Fragment|Wise Dragon Fragments]], and Wise Dragon Armor is one of eight different [[Dragon Armor]] sets.
The player needs at least [[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] to be able to use this set, and if a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs XII (12)]], to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].

## Tips
- Wise Dragon armor with Necrotic Reforge allows for relatively high defense and health, while providing the same amount of effective [[Intelligence]] as [[Crystal Armor|Necrotic Crystal Armor]].
- Note: The Effective [[Intelligence]] is only the same as Necrotic Crystal Armor When the Full Set Bonus Is active.
- It can be a good alternative to [[Adaptive Armor]] for low-budget mages in the catacombs.
- When paired with [[Aspect of the End]], players can gain massive boost to their mobility, before gaining access to more late-game sources of increasing the efficiency of the weapon's ability.
- Pairing this armor set with a [Lv100] [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sheep Pet]] effectively gives the player the same [[Mana]] cost reduction as the pre-0.7.5 ability.
- Due to its [[Intelligence]] being very good even without the set bonus, players seeking ability damage should swap the helmet with [[Dark Goggles]] or its upgrades.
## Notes
- Rather than reducing the [[Mana]] cost of items by 33%, the reduction is exactly 1/3.
- This is the best armor set for abilities as the full set bonus effectively increases the Mana Pool by 50% by lowering costs with 1 third while keeping the cost of all [[Power Orbs]] down.
- All abilities that reduce [[Mana]] cost of items are multiplicative with each other, as additive stacking would allow them to become 0 or lower.
  - However, it is possible to have negative cost with items that directly provide [[Mana]], such as [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin Boots]] and [[Power Scrolls|Sapphire Power Scroll]].
## Trivia
- This armor is one of the four Dragon Armors that has coloring similar to that of the main stat bonus. The light blue color of the armor is the same light blue color of the [[Intelligence]] stat. The other three armors that have similar colors to their stats are [[Strong Dragon Armor]], [[Young Dragon Armor]], and [[Holy Dragon Armor]].
- If one applies the [[Reforging|Wise]] Reforge to the armor, it's name becomes Very Wise Dragon Armor. Other items with similar effect can be seen [[Reforging|here]].

# Wisp Spirit Skin
Aliases: Wisp Spirit Mask Skin
Categories: Legendary items, Helmet Skins, Seasonal Bundles, Pages with UIs
[Wisp Spirit Skin]
Type: Helmet Skin ([[Helmet Skins]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Item skins give your gear a fresh new look! Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to Spirit Mask and  Spirit Mask.; This skin is swappable between 21; wispy spirited variants!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Wisp Spirit Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Wisp Spirit Skin can be purchased from the Fall 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Wisp Spirit Skin can be applied to a [[Spirit Mask]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 21 wispy spirited variants!

The Wither Relic is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that reduces the damage taken from [[Wither]] mobs by 25%.
## Obtaining
### [[Crafting]] Recipe
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Wither Artifact]] 8x [[Wither Catalyst]]|Recipe: [[Wither Catalyst]] ×8 + [[Wither Artifact]] → Wither Relic|
## Trivia
- Before 0.11.3, the [[Wither Artifact]] kept its [[Rarity|EPIC]] rarity, making it the only accessory that did not increase its rarity upon being upgraded other than the [[Ring of Love]] line of accessories.
- There was a bug where you could stack the [[Wither Artifact]] with the Wither Relic to get 45% reduced damage taken from Withers.

|Merchant|Ingredients|
|-|-|
|[[Master Tactician Funk]]|1,464,000 [[Coins]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Enchanted Oak Log]] 320x [[Enchanted Birch Log]] 320x [[Enchanted Spruce Log]] 320x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] 320x [[Enchanted Acacia Log]] 320x [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|
## Usage
A Wood Singularity adds +25 [[Foraging Fortune]] to any [[Axes|Axe]] when applied in an Anvil.
Previously, it would apply +100 [[Strength]] to any Wooden Sword, applying the Thick prefix and increasing its rarity as if [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]]. Any Thick Wooden [[Swords]] obtained prior can be refunded at [[Master Tactician Funk]] or in the /reclaim menu.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Annihilation Cloak]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Destruction Cloak]] [[Wood Singularity]] 2x Wood Singularity 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 16x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: Wood Singularity ×2 + [[Destruction Cloak]] + [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×4 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 + [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] x16 → [[Annihilation Cloak]]|
|[[Destruction Cloak]]|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Wood Singularity]] 3x Wood Singularity 8x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: Wood Singularity ×3 + [[Molten Powder]] x48 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] x4 ×2 → [[Destruction Cloak]]|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Cayenne IV ([[Cayenne]]))|Chili Pepper V (5) ([[Chili Pepper]])|[[Wood Singularity]] 1x Wood Singularity 4x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 32x [[Chili Pepper]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×4 + Wood Singularity → [[Cayenne]]|
## Trivia
- Wooden Singularities were previously applicable to all wooden swords, granting +100 [[Strength]] and applying the Thick prefix. It was applicable to the [[Wooden Sword]], [[Aspect of the Jerry]], [[Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition|Aspect of the Jerry Signature Edition]], [[Scorpion Foil]], [[Tactician's Sword]], [[Sword of Revelations]], [[Sword of Bad Health]], and [[Great Spook Sword]].
- Applying a Wood Singularity on an [[Aspect of the Jerry]] previously granted the [[Achievements|"Seriously?" Achievement]]. It is now obtained by applying a [[Recombobulator 3000]] on one.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 20, 2025|Changed Wood Singularity to be applied to [[Axes]] instead of Wooden Swords ([[Swords]]).|
|Aug 20, 2025|Added the ability to reclaim Wood Singularity from Wooden Swords ([[Swords]]) at [[Master Tactician Funk]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 11, 2019|0.7.4|Added Wood Singularity.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Added Wood Singularity to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Changed Wood Singularity to be applied to [[Axes]] instead of Wooden Swords ([[Swords]]).|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Added the ability to reclaim Wood Singularity from Wooden Swords ([[Swords]]) at [[Master Tactician Funk]].|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Wood Singularity can now also be reclaimed from Wooden Swords in the /reclaim menu.|

The Wumpa Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cold Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Wumpa Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Wumpa]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Wumpa Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cold Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cold Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Cold Resistance]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Wyvern Dragon Helmet Skin
Categories: Legendary items, Helmet Skins, Pages with UIs

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Item skins give your gear a fresh new look! Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to any type of Dragon Helmet.; This skin is swappable between 21; flying Wyvern variants!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Wyvern Dragon Helmet Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Wyvern Dragon Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Hypixel Store as part of the 2026 Summer Legacy Bundle.
## Usage
The Wyvern Dragon Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Young Dragon Armor|Young Dragon Helmet]], [[Old Dragon Armor|Old Dragon Helmet]], [[Strong Dragon Armor|Strong Dragon Helmet]], [[Wise Dragon Armor|Wise Dragon Helmet]], [[Unstable Dragon Armor|Unstable Dragon Helmet]], [[Protector Dragon Armor|Protector Dragon Helmet]], [[Holy Dragon Armor|Holy Dragon Helmet]] or [[Superior Dragon Armor|Superior Dragon Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 21 flying Wyvern variants!

Item ID: YETI_SWORD
Tooltip: Damage: +150; Strength: +170; Intelligence: +50; Gemstones: [] []; Ability: Terrain Toss RIGHT CLICK Throws a chunk of terrain in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 15,000 damage.; Mana Cost: 250 Cooldown: 1s; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Yeti Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. Its ability throws terrain to deal damage.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[True Ice]] 32x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 384x (6 stacks (384 items total)) [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x64 ×6 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x16 ×2 + [[True Ice]] → Yeti Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Yeti Sword [[Damage]]: +150 [[Strength]]: +170 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Terrain Toss RIGHT CLICK; Throws a chunk of terrain in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 15,000 damage. Maximum range of 32 blocks.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 250; Cooldown: 1s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
Sword:
[[Yeti Sword]] [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +185 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Terrain Toss RIGHT CLICK; Throws a chunk of terrain in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 17,000 damage. Maximum range of 32 blocks.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 250; Cooldown: 1s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
When the item's ability is used, a small piece of terrain is tossed upward towards the player's cursor, exploding on impact. The damage dealt by the explosion scales not only with the player's [[Intelligence]], but also with distance between the mob and the explosion center. The ability costs 250 [[Mana]] and has a cooldown of 1s.
## Upgrading
Upgrading the Yeti Sword grants the following:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +15 [[Strength]]
- Terrain Toss gains +2000 [[Damage]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Yeti Sword]] 1x Yeti Sword 8x [[Winter Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Winter Fragment]] ×8 + Yeti Sword → [[Yeti Sword]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Ice Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|100|100|
|✪|100|200|
|✪✪|150|350|
|✪✪✪|200|550|
|✪✪✪✪|250; 10,000 [[Coins]]|800; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,100; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- If the player is midair when Terrain Toss is used, the game throws a single Packed Ice block.
  - The damaging area remains unaffected.
- The boulder does not cause endermen to attack and thus can be used to kill endermen in the [[Void Sepulture]] safely.
- With a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Baby Yeti Pet|Baby Yeti]] Pet, it is possible to repeatedly use Terrain Toss up to 4 times per second.
- Terrain Toss casts a boulder that scales magic damage by how close the enemy is within the boulder. It will deal the most damage if the center is directly on top of the enemy.

## Trivia
- Young Dragon Armor is one of four dragon armors to have coloring similar to its main stat bonus. The white color of the armor is the same white color of the [[Speed]] stat.
  - The other three armors that have similar colors to their stats are [[Strong Dragon Armor]], [[Wise Dragon Armor]], and [[Holy Dragon Armor]].
- Ignoring the set bonus, Young Dragon Armor grants the second highest [[Speed]] out of any [[Armor]] set, behind [[Maxor's Armor]].
  - Including the set bonus, Young Dragon Armor grants the highest [[Speed]] out of any armor set, giving 30 [[Speed]] more than [[Maxor's Armor]].
## Notes
- The [[Speed]] cap increase stacks with other sources.

# Zorro's Cape
Aliases: ZORROS CAPE, Zoro, Zorro, Zorro's Cloak
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Equipment, Cloaks
[Zorro's Cape]
Type: Cloak ([[Cloaks]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Chocolate Shop ([[Chocolate Factory]])
Stats: [[Strength]]: +10; [[Ferocity]]: +2; [[Farming Fortune]]: +10; [[Farming Wisdom]]: +1
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+15 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ZORROS_CAPE
Tooltip: Strength: +10; Ferocity: +2; Farming Fortune: +10; Farming Wisdom: +1; The stats of this Cape double during Jacob's Farming Contest.; Additionally, you have a 20% chance to obtain an extra medal from contests.; Not all Rabbits wear capes.; Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Zorro in Hoppity's Collection.; LEGENDARY CLOAK
Zorro's Cape is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Cloaks|Cloak]].
## Obtaining
Zorro's Cape can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 20,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] after reaching [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Factory V (5)]] and obtaining Zorro in [[Chocolate Factory|Hoppity's Collection]].
## Usage
Cloak:
Zorro's Cape [[Strength]]: +10 [[Ferocity]]: +2 [[Farming Fortune]]: +10 [[Farming Wisdom]]: +1
[Effects]
[The stats of this Cape double during Jacob's Farming Contest. Additionally, you have a 20% chance to obtain an extra medal from contests.]
The base stats of Zorro's Cape double during [[Jacob's Farming Contest]]: additionally, while equipped, the player gains a 20% chance to obtain an extra medal from [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]]. Zorro's Cape cannot be equipped without having Zorro in [[Chocolate Factory|Hoppity's Collection]].
## Tips
- The [[Blossom Cloak]] has a maximum of 82.5 [[Farming Fortune]], while Zorro's Cape caps at 66 [[Farming Fortune]] (132 [[Farming Fortune]] during [[Jacob's Farming Contest]]). This averages out to 88 [[Farming Fortune]] over the period of an hour. Therefore, if one does not want to constantly swap their cloak, it is best to always use Zorro's Cape.
  - Zorro's Cape multiplies the [[Overbloom]] granted by the [[Blooming Thorns|Thorny Reforge]] during [[Jacob's Farming Contest]]. It can be used to give at most 76 [[Farming Fortune]] and 4.133 [[Overbloom]] when averaged out, which is a significant amount of overbloom.
- The chance to receive an extra medal is rolled when claiming the rewards for a contest.
## Trivia
- Zorro's Cape is most likely a reference to Zorro, a fictional swordsman with a black cape.
  - This also might explain why Zorro's Cape provides [[Strength]] and [[Ferocity]], despite being geared toward farming.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 9, 2024|Added Zorro's Cape.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Zorro's Cape.|

# "Your" Island
Aliases: Your Island
Categories: Locations
["Your" Island]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: -91 20 -27
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Jerry (Rift)]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Tree-like Wool]]
[Metadata]
ID: BACK_2_BASICS
"Your" Island is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Purpose
There are 2 trees on "Your" Island, which are made of [[Oak Log|Oak Logs]], however, when broken, drop [[Tree-like Wool]]. This can be used to bridge from the spawning island to the other, where [[Jerry (Rift)|Jerry]], an Enigma Soul and the portal back to the [[Village Plaza]] can be found.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in "Your" Island:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jerry (Rift)]]|[[Jerry (Rift)]]|"Your" Island; -85.5, 20, 4.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about how they appear everywhere.|✕|
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in "Your" Island:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Back to Basics|-87.5|20|0.5|In "Your" Island next to Jerry ([[Jerry (Rift)]]).|
## Trivia
- The player receives two objectives when they enter "Your" Island, the first being Break a log, and the next You know the rest, referencing the fact that the player has already done what they must do next before.
- The fake sky and clouds made of wool may be a reference to the similar construct shown in Minecraft: Story Mode, where part of a fake overworld made of wool was built in the End: this, in turn, was likely inspired by The Truman Show.
- Due to the [[RemmaH yniT]] using an Iron Axe texture, it can be used to break the trees faster than using other items.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added "Your" Island.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added "Your" Island.|

# Abandoned Quarry
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[Abandoned Quarry]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: -145 149 -30
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Guard Gornum]] [[Ticket Master]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Mithril Grubber]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Aquamarine Dye]] [[Enchanted Mithril]] [[Mithril]] [[Plasma]] [[Raw Cod]]
Resources: [[Cobblestone]] [[Mithril]] [[Nether Quartz]] [[Titanium]]
The Abandoned Quarry is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It is the only place where [[Ores|Pure Quartz]] can generate.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Abandoned Quarry, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Abandoned Quarry
■ Mine Pure Quartz Ore.
■ Fish up Mithril Grubbers.
## Accessing
The Abandoned Quarry can be found by going through the rail system from the [[Upper Mines]], turning left, and going to [[Coordinates|-145, 149, -30]]. Alternatively, the [[Ticket Master]] and [[Station Master]] can transport the player for [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Abandoned Quarry is a place where players can mine [[Ores|Pure Quartz]] for high quantities of [[Nether Quartz]]. Additionally, [[Mithril Grubber|Mithril Grubbers]], [[Mithril Grubber|Medium Mithril Grubbers]], [[Mithril Grubber|Large Mithril Grubbers]], and [[Mithril Grubber|Bloated Mithril Grubbers]] can spawn here, which can be killed for ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]].
[[Guard Gornum]] can be found at the entrance, explaining how the Abandoned Quarry is the only place [[Mithril Grubber|Mithril Grubbers]] can be fished. Additionally, the [[Speckled Teacup]] has a 1 in 10 chance to replace a GREAT CATCH.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Abandoned Quarry:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Guard Gornum]]|[[Guard Gornum]]|Abandoned Quarry; -158.5, 149, -14.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the Mithril Grubbers ([[Mithril Grubber]]) in the Abandoned Quarry.|✕|
|[[Ticket Master]]|[[Ticket Master]]|Abandoned Quarry; -146.5, 149, -30.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to travel to various parts of the [[Dwarven Mines]] in exchange for [[Coins]].|✕|
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in the Abandoned Quarry:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|DM-12|-195|124|-15|In the water pond|✔|

# Abiphones & Co.
Categories: Locations
[Abiphones & Co.]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])

Before we begin, I just want to say: do NOT purchase a counterfeit [[Abiphones|Abiphone Basic]] in the Hub! Abiphones & Co. does NOT deserve your business!

Abiphones & Co. is a large, tall location in the [[Village]]. Home to [[Alda]] and [[Karis]], it serves as a place for players to purchase an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Abiphones & Co., they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Abiphones & Co.
■ Learn about Abiphones from Alda.
■ Purchase your first Abiphone!
## Purpose
Abiphones & Co. is home to [[Alda]], an [[NPC]] who introduces the player to [[Abiphones]] and sells the [[Abiphones|Abiphone Basic]] for 100,000 [[Coins]]. Both the [[Abiphones|Abiphone Basic]] and [[Abiphone Contacts Trio]] are displayed and prompt the player to talk to [[Alda]] and [[Elizabeth]] when interacting with them.

- There is a duck hidden behind vines below Abiphones & Co. at [[Coordinates|69, 73, -67]].

## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Ancient Ruins, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Ancient Ruins

## Purpose
The Ancient Ruins serve as an entrance to the [[Forest Temple]]. To enter it, the player needs to right-click several lanterns in the right order, which depends on the amount of sea pickles near them. The coordinates of the lanterns are as follows:
1. -641, 56, 48
2. -650, 59, 59
3. -618, 66, 54
4. -633, 73, 65
5. -651, 69, 59

# Ant's Cave
Aliases: Ant Cave, Ant Hill, Anthill
Categories: Locations, Confirmations needed
[Ant's Cave]

Ant's Cave is a [[Locations|Location]] found in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It can be accessed from the [[Spring Depths]] in the [[Torrhus Springs]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Ant's Cave, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Ant's Cave
■ Break Anthills and vacuum up Ants!
■ Purchase Minion Upgrade Stones from Ant.
■ Keep an eye out for the elusive Queen Ant!
## Features
Ant's Cave contains [[Ant|Ants]] and [[Queen Ant|Queen Ants]], which spawn naturally or from Anthills. [[Ant|Ants]] and [[Queen Ant|Queen Ants]] can only be damaged and killed by a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]], as they are a [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]].
Anthills have 6 [[Breaking Power]] and a [[Block Strength]] of 3,500. Breaking one spawns 2-4 [[Ant|Ants]] or [[Queen Ant|Queen Ants]]. It is not affected by [[Mining Spread]].

## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Arachne's Burrow, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Arachne's Burrow
■ Home of Arachne.
■ Tread carefully.
## Features
Arachne's Burrow features many attractions.
### Weak Wall
There are [[Cracked Wall|weak walls]] situated at [[Coordinates|-316, 96, -193]] and [[Coordinates|-233, 71, -317]]. They function similarly to other [[Cracked Wall|weak walls]], where the player must place a [[Superboom TNT]] or [[InfiniBoom™ TNT]] adjacent to the wall to break it. After doing so, the player can enter Arachne's Burrow.
### Arachne's Keeper
[[Arachne's Keeper]] is a unique mob that spawns in one of 9 spots in Arachne's Burrow. An [[Arachne's Keeper]] will spawn in one of the spots at random a few seconds after the previous one has been killed. It violently attacks the player but drops [[Arachne's Calling]] and a variety of loot.
### Spider Mound
The Spider Mound is a small structure located in the tunnels of Arachne's Burrow. There is a hole at the top, with a ladder leading down to a small underground room. A [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] can be found here, as well as sanctuary from [[Spider (disambiguation)|Spiders]], as they cannot enter the room.
## Fairy Souls
There are 3 [[Fairy Souls]] found in Arachne's Burrow:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Location|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|SD-6|-294|36|-274|Arachne's Burrow|Inside a boulder, entrance at -291, 51, -271 ([[Coordinates]])|✔|
|SD-7|-309|63|-185|[[Arachne's Sanctuary]]|Inside the wall on a mushroom, walkable with a two block jump|✔|
|SD-8|-309|66|-245|Arachne's Burrow|Inside the wall|✔|

# Arachne's Sanctuary
Categories: Locations
[Arachne's Sanctuary]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat I (1) ([[Combat]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Journey in the Sky ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Arachne's Burrow]]
Coordinates: -282 47 -190
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Spider Tamer]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Arachne]] [[Arachne's Brood]] [[Jockey Skeleton]] Silverfish ([[Silverfish (disambiguation)]]) [[Splitter Spider]] [[Spider Jockey]] [[Weaver Spider]] [[Voracious Spider]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Arachne's Calling]] [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Spider Eye]] [[String]] [[Tarantula Pet]] [[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]]
Arachne's Sanctuary is a sub-location of the [[Spider's Den]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Arachne's Sanctuary, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Arachne's Sanctuary
■ Home of the Keepers.
■ Arachne's Altar.
## Features
Arachne's Sanctuary features a few attractions.
### Arachne's Altar
Arachne's Altar is a structure in the center of Arachne's Sanctuary. It features various podiums where players can either place 1 [[Arachne Crystal]] or 4 [[Arachne's Calling]]. The former spawns the stronger version of [[Arachne]], whilst the latter spawns the weaker.
|Placement|Message Placed by Other Player|Message Played by Oneself|
|-|-|-|
|1/4|☄ [player] placed an Arachne's Calling! (1/4)|☄ You placed an Arachne's Calling! (1/4)|
|2/4|☄ [player] placed an Arachne's Calling! (2/4)|☄ You placed an Arachne's Calling! (2/4)|
|3/4|☄ [player] placed an Arachne's Calling! (3/4)|☄ You placed an Arachne's Calling! (3/4)|
|4/4|☄ [player] placed an Arachne's Calling! Something is awakening! (4/4)|☄ You placed an Arachne's Calling! Something is awakening! (4/4)|
|Arachne's Crystal|☄ [player] placed an Arachne's Crystal! Something is awakening!|☄ You placed an Arachne's Crystal! Something is awakening!|
### Arachne
[[Arachne]] is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] that can be spawned once the player places either 1 [[Arachne Crystal]] or 4 [[Arachne's Calling]] at Arachne's Altar. Once [[Arachne]] has been spawned, all entrances to Arachne's Sanctuary will be closed off with layers of cobwebs.
The boss itself roams around Arachne's Sanctuary, taunting players and inflicting damage onto them. Once [[Arachne]]'s health drops, they summon a group of [[Arachne's Brood|Arachne's Broods]], and once they are killed, drop a variety of loot that players can collect, depending on their position and how much they contributed to spawning the boss.

# Archery Range
Aliases: Target Practice, Target practice
Categories: Locations
[Archery Range]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -55 61 -84
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Jax]]
The Archery Range is a [[Locations|Location]] found in the [[Village]] near [[Rosetta]]. It is where [[Jax]] can be found, and where the player can complete Target Practice.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Archery Range, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Archery Range
■ Talk to Jax to forge special arrows!
## Target Practice
Once having talked to [[Jax]] for the first time, the player can begin Target Practice. There are four tiers, and completing the first three will fully unlock [[Jax]]'s shop.
Target Practice begins once the player stands on the Pressure Plate in the center of the room. The player will have 3 seconds to prepare before the practice starts. There are 16 Redstone Lamps around the room, and the player must shoot all of them in the required time frame without moving off the pressure plate.
Target Practice:
|Tier|Time|Rewards|
|-|-|-|
|Target Practice 1|25 seconds|Target Practice 2 [[Jax]]:; Reinforced Iron Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Gold-tipped Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Redstone-tipped Arrow ([[Arrows]])|
|Target Practice 2|15 seconds|Target Practice 3 [[Jax]]:; Emerald-tipped Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Bouncy Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Icy Arrow ([[Arrows]])|
|Target Practice 3|12 seconds|[[Jax]]:; Armorshred Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Explosive Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Glue Arrow ([[Arrows]]) Nansorb Arrow ([[Arrows]])|
|Target Practice 4|11 seconds|[[Jax]]'s Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]])|
## Tips
- Increasing the player's [[Attack Speed]] will allow the player to shoot arrows faster, thus reducing the time needed to complete a Target Practice.

# Auction House
Aliases: AH, Auction
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Official Buildings
[Auction House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -18 71 -90
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Auction Agent]] [[Auction Master]]
The Auction House (commonly abbreviated as AH) is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Village]], that conducts auctions. It is a Medieval-styled large building, found just west of spawn or to the right of the map.
Auctions are created and bid on by players. Players can set the starting price of the auction and how long it lasts. To browse or create an auction, the player must interact with the [[Auction Master]] or any [[Auction Agent]] [[NPC]] in the Auction House. Alternatively, if you have a [[Booster Cookie]] active, you can use /ah to open the Auction House menu, from anywhere with the exception of a few places such as [[Dungeons]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Auction House, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Auction House
■ Auction off your special items.
■ Bid on other players' items.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Auction Browser
Auction browsing is done in multiple sections:
- [[Weapons]] ([[Swords]], [[Bows]], [[Axes]], Magic Weapons)
- [[Armor]] ([[Helmets|Hats]], [[Chestplates]], [[Leggings]], [[Boots]])
- [[Accessories]] (Talismans, Orbs, Rings, Artifacts, Relics)
- Consumables (Potions, edible items like [[Bottle of Jyrre]], fish themed items like [[Voodoo Doll]], [[Grizzly Salmon]] and the collectable fishes and some seemingly random items like the [[Gloomlock Grimoire]] or [[Power Scrolls]])
- Blocks ([[Dirt]], [[Stone]], materials represented by a Minecraft block)
- [[Tools]] & Misc. ([[Tools]], magic items, staffs, most [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items and any other uncategorized items)
At the bottom of the GUI, there are a few options: Search (Oak Wood Sign), Item Tier (Eye of Ender), and Sort (Hopper).
The Search (Oak Wood Sign) option, when clicked, gives a place to type the name of the item.
The Sort (Hopper) option will show options for filtering: Highest Bid, Lowest Bid, Ending Soon, and Most Bids.
- There is a minor bug with this ability where, if you sort the items by Ending Soon, auctions that are ending in 5 or fewer minutes will be on the last page.
The Item Tier (Eye of Ender) will view the different rarities of items: [[Rarity|COMMON]], [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]], [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], [[Rarity|DIVINE]], [[Rarity|SPECIAL]], and [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]].
The BIN Filter (to the right of Eye of Ender) will switch between viewing Show All (Powered Rail) which shows both auctions and BINs, BIN only (Gold Ingot) which only shows BINs, and Auctions Only (Gold Block) which only shows active auctions. Unlike the other settings, this one can be personalized and does not have a default viewing, meaning it will always automatically go into the mode you were in previously.
The Bid History of an item is shown when clicking on the item in the Browser and hovering the map.
The Auctions Browser defaults to viewing: Weapons, All Tiers, Highest Bid
## Auction Manager
To manage your auctions, click the Gold Horse Armor in the Auctions Master GUI. If you don't have any auctions active, it will automatically show you the Create Auction screen. This will show what item you have on auction and their current bid if you already have auctions active.
### Creating Auctions
To create an auction, click the Gold Horse Armor again at the bottom of the management GUI. This will open up the auction creator. Clicking an item from your inventory will select it as the one to be put up for auction. Clicking the gold bar will let you choose the minimum bidding price. Clicking the clock will let you select the total amount of time players will have to bid on your auction. There are several options from 1 hour to 2 days as well as an option to customize the auction time. If you want to retrieve your item from the top slot, just click it again. Note that if a player leaves this screen (by pressing ESC or E) while an item is in the slot, it will still remain in the slot. You can see it again by going back to the Create Auction screen.
### BIN (Buy It Now)
By pressing the gold ingot next to the arrow, you can switch your item from being an auction to being a BIN. This allows players to instantly buy your items without needing to wait and does not let other players outbid them. Additionally, the 0.13 update introduced a 20 second grace period after creating a BIN auction where one cannot buy the item during the time.
### Fees and Taxes
When creating auctions, one must pay a fee equal to 5% of the starting price plus additional fees depending on how long the auction will last. BIN taxes work similarly but the fee for starting is 2.5% of the item's price if the price is above 100,000,000 [[Coins]], 2% of the item's price if the price is between 10,000,000 [[Coins]] and 100,000,000 [[Coins]], and 1% if it is below 10,000,000 [[Coins]]. When collecting auctions and BINs above 1,000,000 [[Coins]], up to 1% tax is collected. The tax is capped so that it can't bring the amount of coins you collect from the auction below 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
### Auctions Limit
The player may have 14 active auctions with +3 per additional [[Co-op]] member, up to 35 (14 + 3 * 7, for 7 additional players) for a [[Co-op]] of 8 players in total. Active auctions include auctions that have ended but have not been claimed.
If the player tries to create more auctions than the limit on their profile, the game will send the following message: Reached the active auctions limit!
### Other Information
When you get outbid on an auction or someone buys your BIN, it is mentioned in chat. You can click it to view the auction.
Common items cannot have their starting price be 5x higher than their NPC selling price.
To bid on an auction, you must have enough coins to meet the starting amount, or 2.5% more than the most recent bid subtracting the amount you have already bid on it. Click on an item, then click the gold nugget ("Submit Bid") button. This will subtract the amount from your [[Purse]]. If you do not have enough [[Coins]], the gold nugget will instead show a poisoned potato. When one fails to get the highest bid on an auction, their coins are refunded.
- When a listed items time hits 4 minutes 45 seconds it gets delisted from the auction browser
- If an item is bid on when the remaining time is under 2 minutes the time will be set to 2 minutes
## Mayors' Effect
- [[Derpy]] - While elected, the collection fee is quadrupled, but not creation or time fees.
- [[Dante]] - When Dante was elected, there were [[Goons]] posted outside all [[Official Buildings]], including the Auction House. You cannot enter, unless you are wearing a [[Happy Mask]], or have a [[Dante's Talisman]] in your inventory.
  - Sometimes, if you try to jump up where the Auction Master or Auction Agent are located (even with a [[Happy Mask]] or [[Dante's Talisman]]) you may be met with one of the [[Goons]] messages about needing a [[Happy Mask]].
## Trivia
- The lowest possible starting bid you can set for an item is 1 [[Coins|Coin]]. You can bypass the limit of 10 [[Coins]] by using the default starting bid of a stick or something equally worthless.
- The top auctions can be found downstairs; there is a staircase leading downstairs to the left of the [[Auction Master]].
- If you have over 100,000 [[Coins]] to claim, when you hover over the Claim All option, it will say "Wow that's a lot of coins!"
  - Similarly, if you have over 500,000 [[Coins]] to claim, it'll instead say "Wow that's TONS of coins!"
- The highest amount of time you can set an auction to is 336 hours (14 days) with a fee of 55,200 [[Coins]].
- The highest amount of coins you can set an auction to is 50,000,000,000 [[Coins]] with a fee of 2,500,000,000 [[Coins]] (auction) or 1,250,000,000 [[Coins]] (BIN). Combining this with the duration fee and the 1M+ tax, the most amount of coins you have to pay to the Auction House is 3,000,055,200 [[Coins]].
  - With Derpy however, the quadrupled taxes would increase the total tax to 4,500,055,200 [[Coins]].
- When you jump into the area where the [[Auction Agent|Auction Agents]]/[[Auction Master|Master]] stands you will take knockback and be launched back into the middle. If you re-enter the booth fast enough (e.g. with Jump Boost or an Aspect of the End) it will teleport you to the entrance of the Auction House.

# Backwater Bayou
Aliases: Backwater, Bayou
Categories: Locations
[Backwater Bayou]
Type: Fishing Island ([[Fishing]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to the Bayou]]
Level: Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])
Fairy Souls: 5
← Previous Location: [[Fisherman's Hut]]
Next Location →: [[Lotus Atoll]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Captain Baha]] [[Carly]] [[Hattie]] [[Jonesy]] [[Junker Joel]] [[Researcher Gilbert]] [[Roddy]]
The Backwater Bayou is a [[Fishing]] [[Locations|Location]] and the first island that can be accessed from the [[Ship|Ship Navigator]]. It is unlocked by reaching [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]] and speaking to [[Captain Baha]] in the [[Fishing Outpost]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Backwater Bayou, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Backwater Bayou
■ Talk to Junker Joel about Junk, and how to trade it for valuable items!
■ Add and remove Rod Parts at Roddy.
■ Fish Bayou Sea Creatures.
■ Learn about Fishing Hotspots from Hattie.
## Features
### Chapters
The Backwater Bayou contains [[Chapters]], which consist of 38 tasks for the player to complete. Completing all these tasks rewards the [[Ship|Pearl of the Swamp]], which grants a permanent +1 [[Treasure Chance]]. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Jonesy]].
Begin your journey through the Seven Seas.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Learn About Junk ([[Junk Sinker]])|[[Junker Joel]] will teach you about how to fish Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]).|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])|[[Roddy]] can apply [[Rod Parts]], which can help with customizing your fishing rods.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Learn About Hotspots ([[Hotspot (disambiguation)]])|[[Fishing Hotspots]] grant bonus stats when fishing in them, and allow you to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]). They can be found around many islands.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Pearl of the Swamp (Ship); +35 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
### Roddy
[[Roddy]] can be found here, who can help apply [[Rod Parts]] to [[Fishing Rods]]. To unlock his [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]], one must give him a [[Torn Cloth]].
An example of a hotspot in the Backwater Bayou.
### Hotspots
[[Fishing Hotspots|Hotspots]] appear in water to grant buffs and new [[Sea Creatures]] when the player's bobber is within.
### Junk Fishing
Junk can be caught while a [[Junk Sinker]] is equipped on a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]]. It replaces all Treasure caught. The following items are classified as Junk:
- [[Rusty Coin]]
- [[Busted Belt Buckle]]
- [[Old Leather Boot]]
- [[Moby-Duck]]
### Bayou Sea Creatures
The Backwater Bayou has exclusive [[Sea Creatures]], including the following:
- [[Alligator]]
- [[Banshee]]
- [[Bayou Sludge]]
- [[Dumpster Diver]]
- [[Titanoboa]]
- [[Trash Gobbler]]

# Bank
Aliases: Bank Upgrade, Interest
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Templates with deprecated argument names, Official Buildings, Village
[Bank]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -20 71 -65
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Banker]]
The Bank is a location in the [[Village]] where the [[Banker]] resides. Talking to them will allow the [[Player]] to make interactions with their account, including withdrawals and deposits as well as allowing the [[Player]] to interact with their [[Personal Vault]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Bank, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Bank
■ Talk to the Banker.
■ Store your coins to keep them safe.
■ Earn interest on your coins.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Banking System
Right-clicking a [[Banker]] will open the Bank interface. The player will be presented with the following options:
- Deposit [[Coins]]
  - Options available while depositing [[Coins]]: depositing the whole [[Purse]], half the purse, or any specific amount.
- Withdraw [[Coins]]
  - There are four options available while withdrawing [[Coins]]: withdrawing everything on the account, half the account, 20% of the account, or a specific amount.
- Transaction History
  - Hovering over the map will display information about the last 10 transactions done by the player and their co-op members, including the amount deposited/withdrawn/gained-with-interest and the time since.
- Bank Upgrades
  - This option is not initially visible and is only unlocked after the player has at least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] in their bank at once for the first time.
At the end of every in-game season (31 hours), the player receives interest on their stored [[Coins]].
The first time you deposit coins in the bank, you get 10 free [[Coins]], for finishing the [[Banker|Saving Up]] quest.
[[Coins]] stored in the Bank are not lost when the player [[Death|dies]].
### Bank Upgrades and Interest
Players are able to upgrade their bank account with [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] after meeting the respective requirement in the [[Gold Ingot|Gold]] Collection. Upgrading the Bank account increases the maximum amount of [[Coins]] able to be stored in the bank, and increases the maximum interest able to be gained for all players on the profile. Upgrading Bank accounts also rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
At least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] must be stored in the bank and the [[Purse]] combined before the upgrade screen is unlocked.
Players also earn Interest that's proportional to their Account and the amount of stored [[Coins]]. It can be further increased through [[Museum|Museum Milestones]]. Note: Interest is applied in brackets. As an example, if a player has 15,000,000 [[Coins]] in a Starter Account, interest would be applied in the following manner:
- 2% interest is applied to the 10,000,000 [[Coins]] (for 200,000 [[Coins]])
- 1% interest is applied to the next 5,000,000 [[Coins]] spanning from 10 to 15 million (for 50,000 [[Coins]])

For players in a [[Co-op]], their Personal Bank account (not to be confused with [[Personal Bank]]) also scales with the profile's Bank Upgrades.
The balance limit and the increments for interest are 1/5th of the Co-op Bank Account. The specific values are:
- Starter: 10M [[Coins]] balance limit, 2% interest on first 2M [[Coins]]
- Gold: 20M [[Coins]] balance limit, 1% interest between 2M–4M [[Coins]]
- Deluxe: 50M [[Coins]] balance limit, 0.5% interest between 4M–6M [[Coins]]
- Super Deluxe: 100M [[Coins]] balance limit, 0.2% interest between 6M–10M [[Coins]]
- Premier: 200M [[Coins]] balance limit, 0.1% interest between 10M–32M [[Coins]]
- Luxurious: 1.2B [[Coins]] balance limit, 0.01% interest between 32M–1B [[Coins]]
- Palatial: 12B [[Coins]] balance limit, 0.001% interest between 1B–11B [[Coins]]
In order to fully upgrade the Bank account, a total of 390,000,000 [[Coins]] and 376 (5 stacks plus 56) [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] () are required.
#### Bank Interest Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Bank Interest
 template  = Template:Calculator/Bank Interest
 form      = bankInterestCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = balance|Balance|15000000|int|0-60000000000
 param     = account|Account||select|Starter, Gold, Deluxe, Super Deluxe, Premier, Luxurious, Palatial
 param     = museumMilestone|Museum Milestone|5|int|0-99
 param     = coop|Co-op Personal Bank|false|check
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Locations
The Bank can be accessed through [[Banker]] NPC in [[Village]], or their equivalents:
- [[Banker Barry]] in [[Jerry's Workshop]]
- [[Banker Bonsai]] in [[Tangleburg Bank]]
- [[Banker Broadjaw]] in [[Dwarven Village]]
- [[Belanor]] in [[Scarleton]]
- [[Bruto]] in [[Dragontail]]
The Bank can also be accessed by the [[Player]] even when they are not at any of its physical locations. The [[Emerald|Emerald VI (6)]] rewards the [[Personal Bank|Personal Bank Upgrade]], which allows the [[Player]] to interact with the bank through the [[SkyBlock Menu]] or a physical item. The NPC [[Guy]] lowers the cooldown of this from 1 hour all the way down to instantaneous. This cooldown refreshes when talking to any of the Banker NPCs.
### Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Upon Receiving Interest|You have just received [#] coins as interest in your personal bank account!|
|Personal Bank Account Upon Withdrawal|You have withdrawn [#] coins! You now have [#] coins in your account!|
|Personal Bank Account Upon Deposit|You have deposited [#] coins! You now have [#] coins in your account!|
|[[Co-op]] Bank Account Upon Withdrawal|Withdrew [#] coins! There's now [#] coins left in the account!|
|[[Co-op]] Bank Account Upon Deposit|Deposited [#] coins! There's now [#] coins in the account!|
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Bank:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Banker]]|[[Banker]]|Bank; -29.5, 72, -38 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access the Bank to store or access their [[Coins]].|✕|
## Trivia
- Assuming max interest and a cost of 200,000 [[Coins]] per [[Enchanted Gold Block]], the time taken for each bank upgrade to pay off is as follows. The first bank upgrade takes about 135 days to pay off; the second upgrade takes about 284 days; the third takes upgrade 910 days; and the final upgrade takes 846 days to pay off (less than that of the third upgrade). Note no matter what upgrade the player has, once the player buys an upgrade, these will constantly be working towards making money back.
  - Once the bank upgrade pays off, the player will start making a profit equal to the difference between the tier purchased and the previous tier every 31 hours.
- As of Version 0.27, players can get up to 72% more interest through [[Museum|Museum Milestones]].

Barrier Street is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Auction House]].
## Features
Barrier Street features one main attraction. The player must have secured the [[Globulate Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 10:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location.
### Shen's Auctions
Every SkyBlock Year, a new auction will start. Players can bid on items that are being auctioned on Shen's Auction with [[Motes]]. The top bidders on items can claim the items after the auction has ended. Players who did not win can reclaim their [[Motes]].
Auctioned Items (Shen's Auction):
|Slot|Name|Amount of winning players|
|-|-|-|
|[[Dead Cat Food]]|[[Dead Cat Food]]|60|
|[[Flex Helmet]]|[[Flex Helmet]]|15|
|[[Placeable Fairy Soul]]|[[Placeable Fairy Soul]]|15|
|[[Punchcard Artifact]]|[[Punchcard Artifact]]|25|
|[[Rift Prism]]|[[Rift Prism]]|25|
|[[Ancient Elevator]]|[[Ancient Elevator]]|15|
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Barrier Street:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Damia (Rift)]]|[[Damia (Rift)]]|Barrier Street; 30.5, 72, -94.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Runs the [[Rift Dimension]]'s version of [[Shen's Auction]].|✕|
## Trivia
- Prior to 0.20.3, [[Diaz]] had a perk named "Barrier Street".
- There is an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] located on top of Barrier Street building, which is hidden in a [[Block of Gold]] until a player has completed the [[Fleespook]] section in [[Village Plaza]].

Barry Center is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Community Center]].
## Features
Barry Center features a few attractions.
### Protestors
There are 7 protestors that can be found in the Barry Center. After reporting their complaints to [[Barry]], the player must then resolve the complaints. After doing so, each protestor will move to the [[Barry HQ]].
### Detransfigured Seraphine
After resolving all the protestors complaints, [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] will appear. She will ask the player to transfigure her, at which point the player must splash Seraphine with an [[Anti-Morph Potion]]. After doing so, she will turn into [[Detransfigured Seraphine|Transfigured Seraphine]] and offer the player a [[Used Detransfigured Face]].

At the back of Barry HQ is the Old Election Box, which contains the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]. It can be purchased for 16 [[Scribe Crux|Scribe Cruxes]] and a [[Barry's Montgray Pen]].

# Belly of the Beast
Categories: Locations, Crimson Isle
[Belly of the Beast]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Always Nether ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Heavy Pearl]]
Belly of the Beast is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Crimson Isle]] where [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] can be collected.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Belly of the Beast, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Belly of the Beast
■ Collect Heavy Pearls!
■ Find a way to escape!
■ Don't get digested!
## Description
The Belly of the Beast is accessed by being eaten by [[The Matriarch]], which is located in the [[Matriarch's Lair]].
The Matriarch makes 3 [[Heavy Pearl]] every 24 hours. If The Matriarch has 1 or more Heavy Pearls available, it will attempt to pull the player towards it and eat them. The player will then be transported to the Belly of the Beast. While in the Belly of the Beast, the player's stats are set to their default values and all effects are disabled. The player will be able to collect [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] by hitting piles of them located around the area. Each pile gives 1 Heavy Pearl, and the player will be able to collect the amount of Heavy Pearls that are available. At the same time, stomach acid will rise in the room, dealing heavy damage if the player touches it. The player can escape the Belly of the Beast by punching the towers of Pigs hanging from the top of the area. You do not need to obtain all of the heavy pearls if you want to escape. If the player dies to The Matriarch, there will be a 10 minute cooldown before they can be eaten again.
A Heavy Pearl being Obtained
The view from its throat upon entering
## Trivia
- It is most likely inspired by the Sarlacc from Star Wars, a tentacle beast with a similar function but without the Pearls.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Unknown Date|Added Belly of the Beast.|

# Birch Park
Categories: Locations
[Birch Park]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): Travel Scroll to the Park ([[Travel Scrolls]])
Fairy Souls: 3
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Park]]
Requirements: Foraging Tutorial ([[Quests]]) Quest
← Previous Location: [[Forest]]
Next Location →: [[Spruce Woods]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Charlie]] [[Vanessa]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Squid]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Aquamarine Dye]] [[Enchanted Ink Sac]] [[Ink Sac]] [[Lily Pad]] [[Squid Boots]]
Resources: [[Birch Log]] [[Charlie's Trousers]] [[Travel Scroll to The Park]]
The Birch Park is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Birch Park, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Birch Park
■ Talk to Charlie.
■ Chop down Birch logs.
## Features
There are various mechanics active in the Birch Park, and affect some aspects of gameplay.
### Charlie
[[Charlie]] is an [[NPC]] located right in front of the [[Launch Pad]] to the Birch Park, when the player arrives from the [[Forest]]. They are part of the "Into the Woods" [[Quests|Quest]], and require the player to chop down 64 (1 stack) [[Birch Log|Birch Logs]]. The player will be rewarded for completing the quest with [[Charlie's Trousers]], a [[Travel Scroll to The Park]], 1,000 [[Coins]], +250 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], and access to the [[Spruce Woods]].
### Rain
[[Vanessa]] can be paid 5,000 [[Coins]] to make the it rain in the Birch Park for 60s, with the limit of 30m.
While raining, players gain +25 [[Fishing Speed]]. Additionally, every 11 seconds there's a 66.6% (2 in 3 rolls) chance to spawn 1-2 [[Squid|Squids]] in water if there's less than 5 [[Squid|Squids]] alive.
### Flower Pot
In what looks to be a ruined building, there is a [[Flower Pot]] with a Torchflower that, when clicked, emits particles and makes a noise. This noise increases in pitch the more one holds left or right-click. Clicking without a flower emits a different sound and particles.
The only known use for this flower pot is to obtain the [[Achievements|"Empty Flower Pot?" Achievement]], which is obtained when one hold a left or right-click on it with any [[Flowers|flower]] for over 5 minutes until the pitch of the sound hits its peak.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Birch Park:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Charlie]]|[[Charlie]]|Birch Park; -277.5, 80, -17.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Rewards the player with [[Charlie's Trousers]] and the [[Travel Scroll to The Park]] after providing 64 Birch Logs ([[Birch Log]]).|✕|
|[[Vanessa]]|[[Vanessa]]|Birch Park; -304.5, 76, -79.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells rain for 5,000 [[Coins]] a minute.|✔|
## Fairy Souls
There are 3 [[Fairy Souls]] found in the Birch Park:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Park-1|-292|69|13|Drop down behind the Diorite Rock|✔|
|Park-2|-305|70|-34|Behind the Waterfall at the pond|✔|
|Park-3|-305|75|-83|Behind [[Vanessa]]|✔|

- A topdown view of fake version of the Birch Park seen from the Hub and Spider's Den up to 0.24.1. A topdown view of fake version of the Birch Park seen from the [[Hub Island|Hub]] and [[Spider's Den]] up to 0.24.1.

- New Mysterious Flower Pot in the Birch Park.

# Black Lagoon
Categories: Locations, Confirmations needed, Templates with deprecated argument names
[Black Lagoon]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Sub-locations: [[Lagoon Cave]] [[Lagoon Hut]] [[Leeches Lair]]
Enigma Souls: 5
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
Coordinates: -119 69 28
← Previous Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Next Location →: [[West Village]]
[Inhabitants]

Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Deadgehog]] [[Lagoon Leech]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Deadgehog Spine]] [[Lil' Pad]]
The Black Lagoon is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It can be entered through the [[Wyld Woods]] for 4:00 [[Rift Time]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Black Lagoon will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Slay Deadgehogs
⇨ Defeat the Leech Supreme
⇨ Eye: The Gillman
⇨ Enigma: Lagoon Cave
⇨ Enigma: Mushroom Guy
⇨ Enigma: Spinning Runes
⇨ Montezuma's Third
Enigma Souls: 5/5
[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Features
### Mushrooms
There are Red and Brown Mushrooms located around the Black Lagoon. When a player is within a few blocks of a mushroom, it will grow into a large mushroom, which the player can use to defend against the attacks of [[Deadgehog|Deadgehogs]].
### Lagoon Current
If the player attempts to cross the lagoon to the [[Lagoon Hut]] by swimming, a current will push them away. The player must use [[Lil' Pad|Lil' Pads]] to cross.
### Lagoon Leeches
If the player goes into the lagoon, a [[Lagoon Leech]] will spawn. The player can stand on [[Lil' Pad|Lil' Pads]] to prevent Lagoon Leeches spawning.

|[[Reed]]|[[Reed]]|[[Lagoon Hut]]; -212, 72, 60.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Rift Boat]].|✔|
|[[Roy]]|[[Roy]]|[[Lagoon Hut]]; -204.5, 75, 49 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells an Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]]) for 4,000 [[Motes]] and 8 Deadgehog Spines ([[Deadgehog Spine]]).|✕|

# Blazing Volcano
Aliases: Cataclysmic, Eruption, Volcanic Eruption, Volcano
Categories: Locations, Crimson Isle
[Blazing Volcano]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Sub-locations: [[Odger's Hut]]
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: "Fires of Fishermen ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
The Blazing Volcano is a Sub-[[Locations|Location]] at the center of the [[Crimson Isle]]. It's purpose is mainly for lava-fishing, as it contains many different sized lava pools. [[Odger]] can be found here and he sells different fishing gears for the player to lava fish there.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Blazing Volcano, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Blazing Volcano
■ Fish for rare loot and find epic secrets.
■ Watch out for the lava.
## Description
The Blazing Volcano can be found by going directly forward at spawn. There is a fortress like Entrance Gate at the base of the Volcano. Volcanic Geysers can be found around the [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] at the bottom of the volcano.
### Odger's Hut
[[Odger's Hut]] is located in the top of the Volcano. It is an obsidian house with a netherbrick roof. To access it, the player must step on the pressure plate, to open the iron door.
### Magma Chamber
Around the Lava Pool at the bottom of the Volcano will be an opening on the lava surface which leads to the [[Magma Chamber]].
## Mechanics
### Geysers
1 Geyser is located in the Blazing Volcano. [[Trophy Fish|Steaming-Hot Flounders]] can be caught when fishing within 2 blocks of the Geyser. Every 10 minutes, the Geyser's location will change to one of the following locations:
- [[Coordinates|-397, 118, -772]]
- [[Coordinates|-390, 118, -754]]
- [[Coordinates|-386, 118, -795]]
- [[Coordinates|-377, 118, -767]]
- [[Coordinates|-365, 118, -761]]
- [[Coordinates|-360, 118, -782]]
- [[Coordinates|-357, 118, -797]]
- [[Coordinates|-344, 118, -786]]
- [[Coordinates|-349, 118, -766]]
### Volcanic Eruption
Approximately every 2-2.5 hours, the lava pool at the bottom will rise, until it completely fills the volcano, when it will start spewing out blocks. If players touch the risen lava without [[Hot Chocolate|Obsidian Skin]], they die. They have different chances to be spewed out.
|Block|Chance|
|-|-|
|Stone|10%|
|Sponge|15%|
|Coal Block|15%|
|Coloured Wool|15%|
|Stained Hardened Clay|20%|
|Obsidian|25%|
Wherever the [[Sponge]] lands, it will turn into [[Ores|Sulphur Ore]], which can be mined with a tool with 8 [[Breaking Power]] or above. Wherever a [[Block of Coal]] lands, it will turn into a [[Matcho]].
#### Explosivity
A volcano eruption also has a chance to have different explosivity levels.
|Name|Chance|
|-|-|
|CHILL|27.12%|
|LOW|23.75%|
|DISRUPTIVE|20.34%|
|MEDIUM|16.95%|
|HIGH|6.78%|
|EXPLOSIVE|3.39%|
|CATACLYSMIC|1.69%|
This system functions similarly to the way the old explosion in the [[Blazing Fortress]] worked, and similarly can be viewed in the tab list.
When the volcano isn't erupting, it will be displayed as INACTIVE.
##### Usage
Chance to spawn a [[Corrupted Mobs|X Corrupted X]] mob is increased to 5% during a Volcanic Eruption.
When the Volcano is erupting, [[Ores|Sulphur Ore]] and [[Matcho]] will spawn around the island. Furthermore, if the eruption is CATACLYSMIC and the player has clicked the bookshelf located on [[Coordinates|10, 127, -938]] with at least 4000 [[Intelligence]], they can — for the duration of the eruption — go near [[Magma Chamber]] and mine a block at [[Coordinates|-373, 82, -880]], with any drill, granting them the [[Kuudra Relic]].
If the player has a full set of [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] they can, during a CATACLYSMIC eruption, speak to [[Drakuu]] and purchase [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]].
## Fairy Souls
There are 2 [[Fairy Souls]] found in the Blazing Volcano:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|CI-10|-396|108|-764|In a hole leading from the Blazing Volcano to the [[Magma Chamber]]|✔|
|CI-11|-343|235|-780|On top of the Blazing Volcano|✕|
## Trivia
- After an eruption, [[Trophy Fish|Gusher]] [[Trophy Fish]] can be caught for 7-16 minutes.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Blazing Volcano.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Blazing Volcano.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Added explosivity level in tab list.|

# Blood God Shrine
Aliases: Shrine
Categories: Locations, Historical content, The Uprising
[Blood God Shrine]
Type: Structure
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -19 68 -111
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Ancient Pig]]
Blood God Shrine was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The Blood God Shrine was a structure that replaced the [[Golden Dante Statue]] after [[Resistance Movement|the Uprising]]. It was located in the [[Village]] at [[Coordinates|-19, 68, -111]], near the [[Librarian]] and [[Blacksmith]]. The [[Ancient Pig]] resides above it, floating with the 7 [[Shiny Relic|Shiny Relics]]. The Blood God Shrine was removed on June 5th 2021.
## Relics Found
|Relic #|Found By|Date Found|
|-|-|-|
|1|carten7|April 17, 2021|
|2|niszczyciel149|April 18, 2021|
|3|The_Blood_Angel|April 21, 2021|
|4|JCRobertson|April 24, 2021|
|5|Jimmicim|April 29, 2021|
|6|lzyy_|April 30, 2021|
|7|MrLopaiz|May 7, 2021|
When found, each Shiny Relic would appear and hover near the shrine with the in-game name of the player who found it.
## Shrine Completion Effects
When all 7 Shiny Relics were found, players were given the [[Pet Items|Flying Pig]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]], which can be given to the [[Pig Pet]].
### Ancient Pig Effects
In addition to the Flying Pig, the Ancient Pig introduced a set of helpful effects. Every 20 real-life minutes (1 SkyBlock day) for the next 2 real-life days after its summoning, it'd randomly pick one to apply for all [[Player|players]]. These effects include:
- Fairy Soul Infusion: Buff stats by 50% for 60s every time a fairy soul is clicked!
- Thunder: A storm has begun bringing along some rare empowered creatures!
- Double Mob Drops: All drops from mobs are doubled!
- Double Pet Stats: All stats received from pets are doubled!
- Increased Runic Chance: Increased the chance for runic monsters to spawn by 5x!
- Mining Experience: Increased Mining experience earned by +25%!
- Foraging Experience: Increased Foraging experience earned by +25%!
- Farming Experience: Increased Farming experience earned by +25%!
- Fishing Experience: Increased Fishing experience earned by +25%!
- Combat Experience: Increased Combat experience earned by +25%!
## Dialogue
Unlike most in-game structures, the shrine could speak, or at least allow players to hear mysterious whispers.
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[SHRINE] Whispers: The resistance leader is a follower of the blood god.|
|None|[SHRINE] Whispers: As mayor, he built this shrine to its magnificence.|
|None|[SHRINE] Whispers: The community shall find the 7 Pig Relics and donate them to the shrine.|
|None|[SHRINE] Whispers: Purchase Shiny Orbs from the Technoshop.|
|None|[SHRINE] Whispers: Use orbs on Shiny Pigs for loot and an RNGesus chance at a Shiny Relic!|
After all 7 Shiny Relics were found, the shrine's dialogue was replaced with the [[Ancient Pig|Ancient Pig's dialogue]].
## Trivia
- When the shrine was introduced, it blocked off the Fairy Soul that was located there. It was later modified to allow access again.
- Before all 7 Shiny Relics were found, a [[Pig]] [[NPC]] that opened the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] resided next to the shrine.
- Some lobbies were glitched during the event, removing the [[Ancient Pig]] and adding the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] back in. The items from the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] could still be purchased.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 16, 2021|Added the Blood God Shrine.|
|Apr 19, 2021|Modified the Blood God Shrine to make its Fairy Soul accessible again.|
|May 8, 2021|Added the Ancient Pig's effects.|
|May 8, 2021|Changed the "Fairy Soul Infusion" buff to +50% stats rather than a +100% buff of all stats.|
|Jun 5, 2021|Removed the Blood God Shrine|
Linked from: [[Ancient Pig]], [[Golden Dante Statue]], [[Hub Well]], [[Piggy]], [[Shiny (disambiguation)]], [[Shiny Orb]], [[Shiny Relic]]

The player can access the Book in a Book by clicking on the bookshelf with particles in [[Marcia]]'s flower shop at [[Coordinates|8, 72, -15]]. This will open a book, and upon clicking LIVE IN THE MOMENT, will teleport the player into the Book in a Book.

# Broken Cage
Categories: Locations
[Broken Cage]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -153 70 162
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Porhtal]]
[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_WITHER_CAGE
The Broken Cage is a sub-location of the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on an area of the [[Wilderness]].
## Purpose
### Wither Skulls
There are 5 Wither Skulls scattered across the floor of the Broken Cage, surrounding [[Porhtal]]. Each of them can be clicked to reveal an eerie line of dialogue, and one of them cause an opening in a nearby tree, revealing an Enigma Soul when the player interacts with it.
### Porhtal
Located in the center of the Broken Cage is [[Porhtal]], an [[NPC]] that owns seven rogue eyes that can be found across the [[Rift Dimension]]. Each of them can be calmed to gain a permanent [[Rift Time]] bonus. The player can teleport from [[Porhtal]] or any of their calmed eyes to another to easily travel across areas at the cost of a large chunk of [[Rift Time]]. Note that this cost usually amounts to 10% less than traveling there yourself.
### Game Messages
|Coordinates|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|-156, 69, 174 ([[Coordinates]])|You investigate.. Whatever was caged, it was here not long ago.|
|-149, 70, 168 ([[Coordinates]])|You investigate.. Tremendous power emanates from this location.|
|-157, 70, 165 ([[Coordinates]])|You investigate.. It's a piece from... a wither?|
|-154, 70, 156 ([[Coordinates]])|You investigate.. It looks like something broke out of the cage.|
|-166, 70, 161 ([[Coordinates]])|You investigate.. The caged being could cross dimensional and chronological boundaries with ease.|
|-144, 67, 173 ([[Coordinates]]) (Leads to an Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]]))|You investigate..|
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Broken Cage:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Porhtal]]|[[Porhtal]]|Broken Cage; -157.5, 70.5, 162 ([[Coordinates]])|Unlocks the ability for players to teleport to certain locations in the [[Rift Dimension]].|✕|
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in the Broken Cage:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Wither Cage|-141.5|68|174.5|Revealed after interacting with the Wither Skull at -144.5, 68, 173.3 ([[Coordinates]]) in the Broken Cage.|
## Trivia
- The caged being in question is [[The Wither King]] aided by [[Necron]].
  - The escape is further described in the [[Journal|Dungeon Journal Apprentice 3]].

[Bubbleboost Column]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]])
Music: "Moonglade Marsh ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Coordinates: -575 70 -13
← Previous Location: [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]]
Next Location →: [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]] [[Tangleburg]]
[Metadata]
ID: BUBBLEBOOST_COLUMN
The Bubbleboost Column is a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Bubbleboost Column is a well system located in [[Tangleburg]] that leads to the [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]] and [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]].
## Trivia
- Occasionally, [[Spike|Spikes]] can enter the Bubbleboost Column and travel to [[Tangleburg]].
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Bubbleboost Column.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Bubbleboost Column.|

# Builder's House
Categories: Locations
[Builder's House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -13 70 -59
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Builder]] [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] [[Wool Weaver]]
The Builder's House is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Village]] where the [[Builder]], [[Wool Weaver]], and [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] live.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Builder's House, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Builder's House
■ Purchase blocks for creative projects on your island!
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Builder's House:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Builder]]|[[Builder]]|Builder's House; -8.5, 71, -61.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various blocks that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]].|✔|
|[[Mad Redstone Engineer]]|[[Mad Redstone Engineer]]|Builder's House; -8.5, 71, -55.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells redstone-related items.|✔|
|[[Wool Weaver]]|[[Wool Weaver]]|Builder's House; -17.5, 71, -57.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various types of colored [[Wool]], [[Hardened Clay]], and [[Glass]].|✔|

- The old Builder's House

## Trivia
- Prior to version 0.9.7, the Builder did not have a house. There was only a Builder's Stand. After version 0.9.7, the Builder has new blocks for sale, with new NPCs with their types of blocks to build. The Builder's House was changed again with the hub revamp of 0.24.2.

# Burning Desert
Aliases: Meloncopter
Categories: Locations
[Burning Desert]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])

Music: "Always Nether ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

The Burning Desert is a large area on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Burning Desert, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Burning Desert
■ Visit Dragontail.
■ Collect Red Sand.
■ Survive the wildlife.
## Location
The location features a [[Red Sand]] desert, with boulders and small lava pools. The [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] [[Trophy Fish]] can exclusively caught from [[Fishing]] from lava there. [[Magma Cube Rider|Magma Cube Riders]] and [[Flaming Spider|Flaming Spiders]] naturally spawn in the Burning Desert.
## NPCs

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Captain Ahone]]|[[Captain Ahone]]|Burning Desert; -526.5, 86, -885.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) to find the lost Apprentice ([[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)]]).|✕|
|[[Elle]]|[[Elle]]|Burning Desert; -487.5, 95, -652.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]), when the player has to kill a [[Flaming Spider]].|✕|

|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|CI-12|-500|127|-795|On top of a rock|✕|

- A hidden Melon Helicopter can be found underneath the Burning Desert.
- The Meloncopter

# Cake House
Categories: Locations
[Cake House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 2
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -88 70 -86
[Metadata]
ID: CAKE_HOUSE
The Cake House is a sub-location of the [[West Village]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Features
The Cake House features a few attractions.
### House
There Cake House is full of Cake, which can be eaten to navigate through the house. There are two floors, with a staircase joining the two together. Every single block of air is replaced by Cake, and each one has 7 slices, which once are completely eaten, clears the space entirely.
### Window
On the second floor of the Cake House is a window, also filled with cake. The cake filling the window cannot be eaten, and when the player attempts to do so, will be granted with the following message:
The window cakes are made of metal! Can't break them!
## Enigma Souls
There are 2 [[Enigma Souls]] found in the Cake House:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Cake House #1|-93.5|70|-83.5|On the first floor of the Cake House.|
|Cake House #2|-94.5|76|-81.5|On the second floor of the Cake House.|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Cake House.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Cake House.|

# Canvas Room
Categories: Locations
[Canvas Room]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: 56 22 82
← Previous Location: [[Flower House]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Marco]]
The Canvas Room is located right below the [[Flower House]] and is accessed by a teleport pad in it, next to [[Marco]]. In the Canvas Room the player can draw freely on a wool canvas using a [[Spray Can]]. This will not consume any dye from the Spray Can.
## Purpose
The Canvas Room is a large space underground, with a teleport pad leading back to the [[Flower House]]. There is a platform extends to the center, and stairs leading down on either side. There are flowers decorating the sides of the Canvas Room, and directly in front of the platform is a large wool canvas. The size of the wool canvas in the room is 32 * 32.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Canvas Room:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Marco]]|[[Marco]]|Canvas Room; 59.5, 22, 68.5938 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about future functionality of the Canvas Room.|✕|
## Gallery
- The Canvas Room
- The wall with the white canvas in the Canvas Room

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 13, 2020|0.8.1|Added Canvas Room .|
