# Carnival
Aliases: Catch a Fish, Chivalrous Carnival, Fruit Digging, Zombie Shootout
Categories: Locations, Events
[Carnival]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Carnival ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])" (Idle); "Diggidi Dee ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])" (Fruit Digging); "Targets ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])" (Zombie Shootout); "tuna_sandwich ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])" (Catch a Fish)
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -85 71 -20
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Carnival Cowboy]] [[Carnival Fisherman]] [[Carnival Leader]] [[Carnival Pirateman]] [[Chantelle]] [[Doug]]
The Carnival is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Hub Island]] that appears when [[Foxy]] is [[Mayor Election|elected]] with her Chivalrous Carnival perk: it contains minigames that reward [[Carnival Tokens]], which can be spent from [[Doug]] shop.
Each minigame costs 5 [[Carnival Ticket|Carnival Tickets]] to play, and rewards 1 [[Carnival Tokens|Carnival Token]] for every 100 points.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Carnival, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Carnival
■ Talk to the Carnival Leader.
■ Complete goals in various Carnival games.
■ Upgrade seasonal perks.
## Minigames
The Carnival currently features 3 different minigames. Each has its own designated game area, NPC, and a tool which players use to play. A minigame can be started by talking to an NPC responsible for that game. To start a game, the player must have an empty hotbar slot for the designated tool to be given.
After playing, one can read their yearly and lifetime scores in each game by talking to [[Chantelle]] near the entrance of the Carnival.
|Game|NPC|Location|Tool|
|-|-|-|-|
|Fruit Digging|[[Carnival Pirateman]]|-107, 73, -2 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Carnival Shovel]]|
|Zombie Shootout|[[Carnival Cowboy]]|-103.5, 70, 8.5 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Carnival Dart Tube]]|
|Catch a Fish|[[Carnival Fisherman]]|-81.5, 72, -0.5 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Carnival Rod]]|
### Fruit Digging
The goal is to earn as many points as possible by using the [[Carnival Shovel]] to dig up Fruits scattered in a 7x7 grid of sand, within 5 minutes.
- The grid consists of:
  - 34 Fruits of 8 different kinds, each with different points values and abilities;
  - 10 Bombs which destroy adjacent undiscovered Fruits.
  - 5 Rums which disable the [[Carnival Shovel]] ability for the next dig.
- The [[Carnival Shovel]] can dig 15 times. It has 3 dowsing abilities:
  - Mines tells how many Bombs are adjacent to the block dug.
  - Anchor tells the position of the lowest scoring Fruit near the block dug.
  - Treasure tells the highest scoring Fruit adjacent to the block dug.
|Fruit|Points Given|# Per Board|Ability|
|-|-|-|-|
|Dragonfruit|1200|1||
|Mango|300|10||
|Coconut|200|3|Prevents a Bomb from exploding for the next dig, if dug.|
|Apple|0|8|+100 points per Apple dug OR blown up by Watermelon up to that point.|
|Cherry|200|2|+300 points per Cherry dug OR blown up by Watermelon up to that point.|
|Pomegranate|200|4|Multiplies the points earned from the next dig by 1.5x.; If the next dig is Watermelon, the subsequently revealed fruit(s) are not multiplied.|
|Durian|800|2|Multiplies the points earned from the next dig by 0.5x.; If the next dig is Watermelon, the subsequently revealed fruit(s) are not multiplied.|
|Watermelon|100|4|Reveals a random Fruit in a tile adjacent to itself (or a random tile if all adjacent tiles are destroyed/revealed): granting 0.5x of its point value and activating its ability, even if it is a Watermelon; without consuming a use on the [[Carnival Shovel]]. Its dowsing ability is used after the watermelon chain is revealed.|
### Catch a Fish
The goal is to catch as many Fish as possible using the [[Carnival Rod]] within 100 seconds:
- Fish spawn, swim erratically in a pond before despawning. They are caught by hooking onto them and holding Right-Click to reel them until the progress bar above them fills. The progress bar fills at varying speeds with Red being the fastest and Yellow being the slowest.
- Catch Streak Bonus: 5 bonus points are awarded per catch in a row, up to 50 bonus points per catch. There is also a 10% chance to gain double points. This resets after failing to catch a fish or 3 seconds without hooking onto one.
- The [[Carnival Rod]] has infinite uses.
- The player is immobile.
|Fish Type|Points Given|Overall proportion|
|-|-|-|
|Red|50|60%|
|Blue|100|30%|
|Yellow|200|10%|
### Zombie Shootout
The goal is to gain as many points as possible by shooting Zombies and [[Redstone Lamp|Redstone Lamps]] using the [[Carnival Dart Tube]] within 100 seconds:
- Zombies spawn in waves on 2 platforms of varying heights and distances to the player, walking back and forth before disappearing after a few seconds A yellow timer at appears above their heads before turning red.
- [[Redstone Lamp|Redstone Lamps]] will occasionally turn on for a few seconds throughout the game, accompanied by a sound cue.
- The [[Carnival Dart Tube]] has infinite ammo.
- The player is immobile.
|Target|Points Given|Lifetime|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|Leather Zombie|30|7 seconds|10% chance to spawn as a Baby Zombie, running faster, granting 2x points.|
|Iron Zombie|50|6 seconds|(same as above)|
|Gold Zombie|80|5 seconds|(same as above)|
|Diamond Zombie|120|4 seconds|(same as above)|
|Active Redstone Lamp|100|6 seconds|Gain 1.2x points for the next 6 seconds.|
## Carnival Goals
Each year, players can complete a bunch of additional tasks in minigames in exchange for bonus [[Carnival Tokens]] and [[Carnival Ticket|Carnival Tickets]]. These tasks can be viewed and upon completion claimed by talking to [[Chantelle]].
List of Goals:
|Game|Goal|Rewards|
|-|-|-|
|Fruit Digging|Play 3 games of Fruit Digging.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Fruit Digging|Reach 3,000 score in a single game of Fruit Digging.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Fruit Digging|Dig up 3 Apples in a single game of Fruit Digging.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Fruit Digging|Dig up 5 unique Fruits in a single game of Fruit Digging.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Fruit Digging|Dig up a Dragonfruit.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Catch a Fish|Play 3 games of Catch a Fish.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Catch a Fish|Reach 3,000 score in a single game of Catch a Fish.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Catch a Fish|Catch 30 Fish in a single game of Catch a Fish.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Catch a Fish|Catch 5 Yellow Fish in a single game of Catch a Fish.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Catch a Fish|Reach a 5 Catch Streak in a single game of Catch a Fish.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Zombie Shootout|Play 3 games of Zombie Shootout.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Zombie Shootout|Reach 3,000 score in a single game of Zombie Shootout.|+ 50 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Zombie Shootout|Shoot 60 Zombies in a single game of Zombie Shootout.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Zombie Shootout|Shoot 5 Diamond Zombies in a single game of Zombie Shootout.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|
|Zombie Shootout|Shoot 5 Redstone Lamps in a single game of Zombie Shootout.|+ 100 Carnival Tokens Carnival Ticket x5|

CLICK HERE to claim your rewards! -----------------------------------------------------|

|Entering Carnival area with a [[Mount]] equipped|Your mount has been despawned.|
|Leaving Carnival area with a [[Mount]] equipped|Your [name] has been restored.|
## NPCs
There are 6 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Carnival:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Carnival Cowboy]]|[[Carnival Cowboy]]|Carnival; -103.5, 70, 38.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to play the Zombie Shootout minigame in the Carnival.|✕|
|[[Carnival Fisherman]]|[[Carnival Fisherman]]|Carnival; -81.5, 72, 29.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to play the Catch a Fish minigame in the Carnival.|✕|
|[[Carnival Leader]]|[[Carnival Leader]]|Carnival; -89.5, 71, 11.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Grants the player 25 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]]) every real-life day.|✕|
|[[Carnival Pirateman]]|[[Carnival Pirateman]]|Carnival; -107, 73, 28 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to play the Fruit Digging minigame in the Carnival.|✕|

|[[Doug]]|[[Doug]]|Carnival; -78.5, 70.5, 22.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various [[Carnival Masks]] and perks related to [[Events]] in exchange for [[Carnival Tokens]].|✔|
## Trivia
- Abilities are disabled while in the Carnival, and you cannot access [[Storage]], the [[Ender Chest]], or [[Backpack|Backpacks]] during a Carnival game.
- The player is unable to cast fishing rods (except the [[Carnival Rod]]) in or near the Carnival.
- The player gets the Mining Fatigue effect while playing a Carnival game.
- The Carnival closes 15 minutes before [[Foxy]] leaves office, and players are no longer able to start new games, but they can finish any existing games. [[Doug]] sticks around for 5 more minutes.

# Catacombs Entrance
Aliases: Catacombs Entrance (Zone)
Categories: Locations, Dungeons, Catacombs
[Catacombs Entrance]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Location: [[Coal Mine]]
Coordinates: -75 56 -125
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Malik]] [[Mort]] [[Ophelia]]
The Catacombs Entrance is an area near the [[Coal Mine]] where 3 NPCs can be found. It is one of two ways to start a [[Dungeons]] run, the other being in the [[Dungeon Hub]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Catacombs Entrance, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Catacombs Entrance
■ Join The Catacombs.
■ Talk to Ophelia and Malik.
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Catacombs Entrance:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Malik]]|[[Malik]]|Catacombs Entrance; -80.5, 56, -119.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to upgrade or salvage their items, and grants access to the Wither Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) and Undead Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).|✔|
|[[Mort]]|[[Mort]]|Catacombs Entrance; -88.5, 55, -128.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to enter the [[Catacombs]].|✕|
|[[Ophelia]]|[[Ophelia]]|Catacombs Entrance; -85.5, 55, -139.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells items that can assist in the [[Catacombs]].|✔|
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in the Catacombs Entrance:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|18|-92|59|-138|Opelia's Shop (Look up and click from -91, 55, -137 ([[Coordinates]]))|✔|
## Trivia
- Even if a dungeon is started from the Catacombs Entrance in the main hub, you will spawn in the Dungeon Hub upon completion.

# Cathedral
Categories: Locations
[Cathedral]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Fairy Souls: 3
Music: "Always Nether ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -38 114 -874
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Dean]] [[Elle]] [[Eludore]] [[Mage Guard (Cathedral)]] [[Scholar Alluin]] [[Undercover Agent]]
The Cathedral is a sub-location of [[Scarleton]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Tasks

- There is a bookshelf at [[Coordinates|-10, 127, -938]] in the Cathedral, which if the player has over 4,000 [[Intelligence]], it will give the following message: You use your mana to pull a book out. Most of it is unreadable but you do notice a 2 and a 22...
  - The 2 and 22 can be inserted into the [[Kuudra Archeologist|Kuudra Archeologist's]] math equation to get ∫2222x−2x5+7x3dx, which gives the coordinates to find the [[Kuudra Relic]] at [[Coordinates|-373, 82, -880]].
- According to lore, the Cathedral has now been repurposed as a university, as no one from [[Scarleton]] ever went to pray anymore.

# Cerebral Citadel
Categories: Locations
[Cerebral Citadel]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: -240 18 -63
← Previous Location: [[Walk of Fame]]
Next Location →: [[The Vents]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Shen Albedo]] [[Shen Chaos]] [[Shen Citrinitas]] [[Shen Magnum Opus]] [[Shen Rubedo]]
[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_CEREBRAL_CITADEL
The Cerebral Citadel is a sub-location of the [[Walk of Fame]]. It can be entered by showing [[Obo]] [[Shen's Ringalia]].
## Features
The Cerebral Citadel features a few attractions.
### Shen's Hive Mind
There are 7 stone buttons on the back wall and 2 wooden buttons. The left wooden button resets the sequence, and the right wooden button controls which sequence to play. There are a total of 5 sequences to play, with the third awarding the [[Celestial Timecharm]] and the fifth awarding the [[Golden Belt]].
To complete a sequence, all [[Redstone Lamp|Redstone Lamps]] must be lit up by clicking each lamp's respective stone button: clicking a button while the lamp is off will turn it on and vice versa. Additionally, flipping a lamp will flip other lamps in all sequences except the first. The sequence of lamps needed for each sequence can be seen below:
|Sequence|NPC|Buttons|Reward|
|-|-|-|-|
|First|[[Shen Chaos]]|Activate each lamp|N/A|
|Second|[[Shen Albedo]]|2, 4, 6|N/A|
|Third|[[Shen Citrinitas]]|2, 5, 6, 7|[[Celestial Timecharm]]|
|Fourth|[[Shen Rubedo]]|3, 4, 7|N/A|
|Fifth|[[Shen Magnum Opus]]|3, 5, 6, 7|[[Golden Belt]]|
### Connections
The Cerebral Citadel can be entered through [[The Vents]] from the [[Wizardman Bureau]].
## NPCs
There are 5 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Cerebral Citadel:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Shen Albedo]]|[[Shen Albedo]]|Cerebral Citadel; -260, 18, -68 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Shen]] from the player's dimension.|✕|
|[[Shen Chaos]]|[[Shen Chaos]]|Cerebral Citadel; -260, 18, -68 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Celestial Timecharm]].|✕|
|[[Shen Citrinitas]]|[[Shen Citrinitas]]|Cerebral Citadel; -260, 18, -68 ([[Coordinates]])|Gives the player the [[Celestial Timecharm]].|✕|
|[[Shen Magnum Opus]]|[[Shen Magnum Opus]]|Cerebral Citadel; -260, 18, -68 ([[Coordinates]])|Gives the player the [[Golden Belt]].|✕|
|[[Shen Rubedo]]|[[Shen Rubedo]]|Cerebral Citadel; -260, 18, -68 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the goals of the different Shens ([[Shen]]).|✕|
## Trivia
- Each minigame reveals that multiple Shens live across universes and are gathering together to form a being of unparalleled greatness.
- Each Shen is named after a process in creating the Magnus Opus or Great Work in the context of alchemy, in order to create the philosopher's stone in 4 stages :
  - (1) the nigredo or blackening, (2) the albedo or whitening, (3) the citrinitas or yellowing and (4) the rubedo or reddening.
  - Their colors, however, do not match the stage they're named after.
## History

# Colosseum
Aliases: Colosseum Arena
Categories: Locations, Player Versus Player
[Colosseum]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: 130 68 23
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Romero]]
The Colosseum is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Hub Island]] found south of the [[Fishing Outpost]]. It used to serve as a PvP arena for players to fight against each other until it was disabled on August 5th, 2020.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Colosseum, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Colosseum
■ Behold the destruction of a cruel tyrant.
## Purpose
The Colosseum currently does not serve much of a purpose in comparison to the past. During the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, however, [[Romero]]'s appears in the Colosseum and asks the player for an [[Emerald Blade]].
### Original Colosseum
#### Fighting
The original purpose of the Colosseum was for players to fight in a typical one-versus-one PvP scenario, using their SkyBlock items. Players spawned on opposite ends of the arena floor and would fight until either one player dies, or 5 minutes expire. Players who died would not lose any [[Coins]]. In a couple small areas of the Colosseum, the [[Aspect of the End]] ability was disabled. In some circumstances, players could hide just outside the arena and be invulnerable to other players, but can still hit them with projectiles such as [[Arrows]].
Players could also speak with the [[Gladiator]] to queue into a duel. Once another player joined the queue, they would both be teleported into a private room. There, [[Mana]] was not regenerated, the [[Damage]] dealt was divided by 3, and players could not use the [[Aspect of the End]] ability. Death messages would not show up except for the player and the opponent.
#### Visuals
The Colosseum was mostly built of stone materials and spruce wood, with some stained clay also included. The center of the arena had a fire under orange stained glass.
The exterior featured many large staircases, which lead up to a wooden viewing platform from which players could see fights within the Colosseum happen. There were also two big fires at the entrance.
A set of stairs downwards leads into an underground set of tunnels. These wrapped around the arena floor, and could be followed all the way around to get back to the entrance of the Colosseum. These also had small corridors leading off of them, which lead to a small ruined area covered in vines and flames. A [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] could be found in this area. This room lead off again into another small ruined room, this time featuring collapsed supports made of fence, and a large cobblestone blockade. This acted as a one-way exit out of the ruined area, and back into the main Colosseum tunnels.
The arena floor itself was primarily comprised of dirt, with some stone in the middle. It featured some fire for lighting but was otherwise unremarkable. Players fighting would be spawned on the east and west sides of this arena, leaving the north and south sides empty.
#### Destruction and Decay
During the fight against [[Dante]], the Colosseum was among the buildings destroyed. Unlike other destroyed buildings, however, it did not revert to its original iteration. In early 2022, most of the slime was removed, the flames in front of the Colosseum were extinguished, and foliage took over the ruins. On November 9, 2022, most of the wooden blocks had rotted away, the braziers disappeared, and water had pooled in the center of the floor. By December 4, 2023, more dirt and water had pooled in the center and more trees had grown in and around the arena. With the release of the Hub Revamp, almost no remains of the structure existed anymore.

# Colosseum (Rift)
Categories: Locations, Rift Dimension
[Colosseum]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])

Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

[Resources]

[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_COLOSSEUM
The Colosseum is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]] where the player can fight [[Bacte]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Colosseum will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Defeat Bacte
⇨ Enigma: Full Circle
⇨ Eye: Gooey
Enigma Souls: 1/1
## Features
In order to access the location, the player must have secured the [[Living Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 15:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining. Due to the time dilation, the player loses [[Rift Time]] twice as slowly when in the Colosseum.
### Bacte
The player can spawn [[Bacte]] in the Colosseum by hitting all the [[Globowl|Globowls]] into [[Bacte|B]], who is in the center of the Colosseum.
### Full Circle
By talking to [[Mole (NPC)|Mole]] on one of the Colosseum's pillars, the player can start the Full Circle quest, in which they must jump on every single pillar. The next pillar they haven't yet jumped on will turn green, and once the player has jumped on all of them, [[Mole (disambiguation)|Mole]] will grant access to the Enigma Soul next to them. The player will receive jump boost while going full circle.
## NPCs

NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Combat Settlement
■ Buy Combat Tools from the Weaponsmith and Rosetta.
■ Talk to Jax at the Archery Range to learn about bows and arrows.
■ Learn about Enchanting at the Library.
■ Talk to Maxwell at the Thaumaturgist to learn about Magical Power.
## Purpose
The Combat Settlement contains several attractions.
- [[Rosetta]] and [[Weaponsmith]] can be found here, the former selling early-game weapons and armor, and the latter selling a variety of swords and bows.
- [[Jax]] can be found in the [[Archery Range]], where the player can attempt higher levels of Target Practice in order to unlock better arrows.
- [[Maxwell]], [[Jacobus]], and [[Ozanne]] can be found in the [[Thaumaturgist]]: here, the player learns about [[Accessory Power]], [[Maxwell|Stats Tuning]], and [[Powers]], and can upgrade their [[Accessory Bag]].
- The [[Librarian]] can be found in the [[Library]] and teaches the player about [[Enchanting]].

# Communal Stew
Aliases: Grand Bakery
Categories: Locations
[Communal Stew]
Type: [[Farming]] location
Location: [[Farm]]
Coordinates: 77 72 -81
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Feast Baker Scott]] [[Feast Chef Ted]]
The Communal Stew is an area where the [[Harvest Feast]] is held.
## Location
The Communal Stew is located in the [[Farm]], which is north-east of the [[Village]].
## Features
The Communal Stew features a large bakery and a giant metal pot, which gradually fills up over the course of the [[Harvest Feast]]. [[Feast Chef Ted]] stands by the pot, where players can see which crops are in-season and their [[Harvest Feast|Feast Milestones]].
While [[Finnegan]] is in office with the Grand Feast perk, [[Feast Baker Scott]] appears in the bakery. Players can purchase items from him using [[Kernels]].
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Communal Stew:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Feast Baker Scott]]|[[Feast Baker Scott]]|Communal Stew; 77.5, 72, -83.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells [[Farming]]-related items in exchange for [[Kernels]] while [[Finnegan]]'s Grand Feast is active.|✔|
|[[Feast Chef Ted]]|[[Feast Chef Ted]]|Communal Stew; 84.5, 72, -78.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the [[Harvest Feast]] and Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]]) events, and which crops are in-season as part of it.|✕|
## Trivia
- [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] can get stuck in the Communal Stew pot.

# Community Center
Categories: Locations, Community Center
[Community Center]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Sub-locations: [[Election Room]] [[Security Hall]]
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 0 71 12
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Aatrox]] [[Biblio]] [[Brigette]] [[Candidate Jerry]] [[Clerk Seraphine]] [[Cole]] [[Derpy]] [[Diana]] [[Diaz]] [[Elizabeth]] [[Finnegan]] [[Foxy]] [[Jimmy]] [[Marina]] [[Paul]] [[Scorpius]] [[Security Clerk]] [[Security Sloth]] [[Susan]]
The Community Center is an [[Locations|Location]] found in the [[Village]] and right behind the [[Hub Island]] spawn. Here, players can participate in the [[Mayor Election]], learn about [[City Projects]], buy [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]], and purchase [[Account & Profile Upgrades]] and [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] at [[Elizabeth]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Community Center, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Community Center
■ Participate in the Mayor Elections.
■ Contribute to City Projects.
■ Unlock upgrades, buffs, and cosmetics in the Community Shop.
## Purpose
### Mayor Elections
By using the [[Teleport Pad]] at the back of the Community Center, the player can travel to the [[Election Room]] and vote on one of five Mayors. Each Mayor will have between 1-3 perks, and Mayors with 3 perks have a chance to gain a 4th perk; each Mayor will also have a Minister perk, which they will retain if they are the elected Minister. [[Diaz]]'s Long Term Investment perk guarantees that the next elected Minister will retain all of their perks.
Every 8 SkyBlock Years, a [[Mayor Election|Special Mayor]] will enter the roster.
On three occasions in SkyBlock history, a special one-time candidate entered the roster: [[Dante]], a dictator who was defeated in [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]]; [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]], the [[Resistance Leader]] following the Uprising; and [[Candidate Aura|Aura]], a communist who disbanded the mayor elections and introduced the [[Minister Election]], where players could be voted as Minister.
### Elizabeth
[[Elizabeth]] has several features in the menu, including:
- Viewing [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]]
- Viewing [[City Projects]]
- Purchasing [[Account & Profile Upgrades]]
- Purchasing items from the [[Bits Shop]]
- Buying [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]]
- Purchasing Ranks from the Hypixel Store
While under the effects of a [[Booster Cookie]], players will be able to obtain [[Bits]]. [[Bits]] can be spent in the [[Bits Shop]] for useful items: most notably, [[God Potion|God Potions]], [[Kismet Feather|Kismet Feathers]], [[Enrichments]], [[Abicases]], and other upgrade components.
Fire Sales are events where players can purchase exclusive cosmetics for [[SkyBlock Gems]], like [[Pet Skins]], [[Helmet Skins]], and [[Barn Skins]].
### City Projects
City Projects are global projects that all players across SkyBlock are invited to donate their resources to. Upon the completion of the City Project and the completion of the development of its respective update, an update to the game world is pushed.
Contributing to City Projects will grant [[Fame Ranks|Fame]] and, occasionally, a special bonus related to the project. Contributing enough times will grant a [[Mementos|Memento]].
### Account Security
The Community Center contains [[Susan]], who grants access to the [[Security Hall]] to players who have reached [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 10]] and above. There, players are given a quest from [[Brigette]] to learn about account security.

The Crimson Isle is a [[Combat]] [[Locations|Location]]. It replaces the [[Blazing Fortress]]; however, it is meant to be a more difficult Combat Island than [[The End]], with most of the mobs having several millions of [[Health]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Crimson Isle, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Crimson Isle
■ Battle against brutal mobs!
■ Collect exotic resources like Spores, Thornleaf, Nether Wart, and more!
■ Visit the Dojo.
■ Defeat Kuudra.
## Location
The Crimson Isle can be accessed only after the player has attained [[Combat|Combat XXII (22)]]. It is traveled to by the use of a [[Launch Pad (Structure)|Launch Pad]] found in the back of the [[Spider's Den]].
There are 21 main sub-location: [[Barbarian Outpost]], [[The Bastion]], [[Blazing Volcano]], [[Burning Desert]], [[Cathedral]], [[Crimson Fields]], [[Dojo]], [[Dragontail]], [[Forgotten Skull]], [[Kuudra's Hollow]], [[Mage Outpost]], [[Magma Chamber]], [[Mystic Marsh]], [[Odger's Hut]], [[Ruins of Ashfang]], [[Scarleton]], [[Stronghold]], [[The Wasteland]], [[Matriarch's Lair]], [[Belly of the Beast]], and [[Aura's Lab]].
## Features
### Factions
[[Factions]] are a new feature of the Crimson Isle. Players can gain reputation with Factions by killing mobs or doing quests (including [[Elle]]'s main questline), giving access to special Items and features. There are two factions: the Barbarian Faction and the Mage Faction.
#### Guards
Guards patrol the cities of important Factions. They are passive, but become hostile when attacked.
### Bosses
Minibosses and the Magma Boss spawn around the island. They drop rare loot and spawn quickly after they are killed. Similar to the [[End Stone Protector]] they will leave iron blocks near death. Minibosses include the [[Magma Boss]], [[Mage Outlaw]], [[Bladesoul]], [[Ashfang]] and [[Barbarian Duke X]].
#### Bladesoul
[[Bladesoul]] is the first boss of the Crimson Isle. It spawns in the [[Stronghold]] every 2 minutes after it is killed. It has 50,000,000 [[Health]].
#### Magma Boss
The [[Magma Boss]] will spawn in the [[Magma Chamber]] every 5 minutes after it is killed. It has 200,000,000 [[Health]].
#### Mage Outlaw
The [[Mage Outlaw]] spawns in the [[Courtyard]] near [[Scarleton]] every 2 minutes after it is killed. It has 70,000,000 [[Health]].
#### Barbarian Duke X
The [[Barbarian Duke X]] spawns in the [[The Dukedom]] near [[Dragontail]] every 2 minutes after it is killed. It has 70,000,000 [[Health]].
#### Ashfang
[[Ashfang]] will spawn in the [[Ruins of Ashfang]] every 2 minutes after it is killed. It has 50,000,000 [[Health]], having the least health of all the minibosses, however is a more complicated fight, usually requiring more than one player to defeat it.
### Kuudra
[[Kuudra]] is a 4-player fight against a boss, similar to [[Dungeons]]. It takes place in [[Kuudra's Hollow]], and drops unique loot.
### Vanquishers
Vanquishers are similar to Special [[Zealot|Zealots]] and are mini-withers that have 5,000,000 [[Health]], which have a small chance to spawn after killing any mob in the Crimson Isle. They drop [[Nether Star|Nether Stars]], which are used to buy [[Kuudra Keys]] from the Emissaries in each of the [[Factions]]' settlements.
### Fishing
The fishing mechanic in the Crimson Isle is different from the other islands. [[Fishing Speed]] is capped at 350 instead of 300. [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] is needed to fish in the Crimson Isle as the [[Starter Lava Rod]] has that requirement.
#### Trophy Fishing
When fishing under a condition, Trophy Fishes can be caught. Trophy Fishes comes with a "rarity" of Bronze, Silver, Gold and Diamond. [[Trophy Chance]] can increase the chance of catching Trophy Fishes.
#### Lava Sea Creatures
The Crimson Isle has exclusive [[Sea Creatures]], including the following:
- [[Magma Slug]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXVII (27)]]
- [[Moogma]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXVIII (28)]]
- [[Lava Leech]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXX (30)]]
- [[Pyroclastic Worm]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXI (31)]]
- [[Lava Flame]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXII (32)]]
- [[Fire Eel]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXIV (34)]]
- [[Taurus]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXV (35)]]
- [[Thunder]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXVI (36)]] and Novice Trophy Hunter
- [[Lord Jawbus]] Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XLV (45)]] and Adept Trophy Hunter
Additionally, 6 Lava Sea Creatures are exclusive to [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]].
- [[Fried Chicken]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XIV (14)]]
- [[Volcanic Snail]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XVII (17)]]
- [[Fireproof Witch]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXII (22)]]
- [[Magma Pillar]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXI (31)]]
- [[Fiery Scuttler]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXVII (37)]]
- [[Ragnarok]]: Requires [[Fishing|Fishing XLVII (47)]]

## Trivia
- Before the Crimson Isle was opened on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], its [[Launch Pad (Structure)|Launch Pad]] would display the following text: Opening In .... business days
- The Crimson Isle was the second Alpha Hypixel Network update to feature the Equipment types [[Necklaces]], [[Cloaks]], [[Belts]], [[Gloves]] and [[Bracelets]].
- The Crimson Isle features are the second ever to use [[Loot Sharing]], the first being the [[Ghost]].
  - All of SkyBlock now uses [[Loot Sharing]].
- The Crimson Isle is based on the Vanilla Nether dimension.
  - The name of the island is likely named after the Vanilla Crimson Forest Nether biome.
- The Crimson Isle is believed to be related to the Catacombs.
  - Ashfang and Thorn have a similar attack pattern. Both of them only take damage from hits of their minions' death. They don't attack by themselves. Instead, they fight with minions.
  - Magma Boss and The Wither Lords have a similar attack pattern as well. Both of them spawn crowds of minions that deals massive damage. They also both deal high damage.

# Critter Safari

The Critter Safari is a [[Hunting]] [[Locations|Location]] accessible from the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. After reaching [[Hunting|Hunting XV (15)]], it can be accessed by speaking to the [[Safari Manager]] using [[Safari Tickets]], which are obtained from [[Miria]]'s [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]].
## Accessing
The Critter Safari can be accessed from the [[Safari Manager]] using [[Safari Tickets]]. These come in multiple varieties, obtained from [[Starlyn Contest|Miria's Contests]].
- Basic Safari Ticket: Unlocks the Basic Safari Experience. The player starts with 32 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]].
- Economy Safari Ticket: Unlocks the Economy Safari Experience. The player starts with 40 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] and gains +10% [[Safari Essence]] and +5 [[Hunting Wisdom]].
- Premium Safari Ticket: Unlocks the Premium Safari Experience. The player starts with 48 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] and gains +25% [[Safari Essence]] and +15 [[Hunting Wisdom]].
- First-Class Safari Ticket: Unlocks the First-Class Safari Experience. The player starts with 56 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]], gains +50% [[Safari Essence]], +25 [[Hunting Wisdom]], and gains a random Quest Item upon entering the Critter Safari.
Higher tiers of [[Safari Tickets]] are obtained from reaching higher brackets in [[Starlyn Contest|Miria's Contest]].
When the player runs out of [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]], they are given the option to leave the Critter Safari.
## Biomes
The Critter Safari is split into 4 biomes: the Cavern, Forest, Haunted, and Icy biomes. Each biome has their own sets of [[Critters]], Floor Drops, and mechanics.
[[Floor Drops]] can be found on the ground, marked by String. They will grant either [[Safari Essence]], [[Hunting|Hunting XP]], a shard, or a random Quest Item, which can be used throughout the Critter Safari to interact with the map.
### Cavern
The Cavern biome consists of a large cavern system that stretches into the bowels of the map, with large veins of Raw Gold, carts full of ores, and mining platforms.
The following [[Critters]] can be hunted here: [[Cavernfish]], [[Flitter]], [[Shyworm]], [[Driftling]], [[Chuckwalla]], [[Rockmite]], [[Scrappy]], [[Snoozle]], and [[Gemzie]].
### Forest
The Forest biome consists of a sprawling network of trees and a small underground cave system.
The following [[Critters]] can be hunted here: [[Foxtrot]], [[Bluebird]], [[Honeybug]], [[Treefrog]], [[Woodchucker]], [[Fluffling]], [[Hideonfloor]], [[Parakeet]], and [[Macaw]].
The Forest also contains the Birdfeeder, which allows players to summon the [[Bluebird]], [[Parakeet]], or [[Macaw]] using foods found from Floor Drops found throughout the Forest biome. Different foods buff different bird chances.
The Birdfeeder can be found at [[Coordinates|-1, 90, 44]].
### Haunted
The Haunted biome consists of a large haunted mansion and a small underground cave to spawn the [[Doomspiral]].
The following [[Critters]] can be hunted here: [[Areita]], [[Bloodbat]], [[Duplico]], [[Gazer]], [[Litterbug]], [[Solsnatcher]], [[Gimmiegold]], [[Hideonwall]], [[Hideyho]], and [[Doomspiral]].
### Icy
The Icy biome features the same [[Cold]] mechanic from the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. This can be decreased while near campfires. [[Cold Resistance]] applies in the Icy biome, and being in water will cause [[Cold]] to increment faster.
The following [[Critters]] can be hunted here: [[Strongarm]], [[Tepid]], [[Polaris]], [[Shuddersquid]], [[Billygoat]], [[Mantis Shrimp]], [[Nozzlenose]], [[Troodon]], and [[Wumpa]].
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Reaching -25 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's getting really cold in here! But you've got to keep moving...|
|Reaching -50 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's so cold that you can barely feel your fingers. Moving is getting difficult...|
|Reaching -75 [[Cold]]|BRRR! Your movement slows to a crawl as the cold threatens to take over. Time to get out of here...|
|Reaching -75 [[Cold]]|The following title also appears:FROSTBITE! Find warmth before you freeze to death...|
|Reaching -90 [[Cold]]|BRRR! You're freezing! All you can think about is getting out of here to a warm campfire...|
|Reaching -100 [[Cold]] and dying, in the Icy biome of the Critter Safari|You fainted and lost some of your items!|
## Critters
37 [[Critters]] are native to the Critter Safari, with 10 Critters being found in the Haunted biome and 9 Critters being found in the others. They can only be hunted with [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]], which are thrown at Critters. Regular [[Hunting Toolkit|Hunting Tools]], such as [[Huntaxes]], [[Black Holes]], [[Lassos]], and [[Fishing Nets]] are disabled in the Critter Safari: if the player ever finds a way to have a Hunting Tool in their inventory, it will be automatically sent to the [[Stash]].
The higher the rarity of the [[Attributes|Shard]] dropped by the player's desired Critter, the lower the chance to successfully capture the Critter.
|Critter Rarity|Successful Catch Chance|Hard Pity|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|100% (1 in 1)|1|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|75% (1 in 1.33)|2|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|50% (1 in 2)|3|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25% (1 in 4)|5|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5% (1 in 20)|20|
If a critter is caught, every player in the Critter Safari receives the shard and progress towards [[Andrea|Safari Milestones]], but not the [[Critterdex]].
Each Critter has a Uniqueness Score, granting +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] for each Critter caught, up to +185,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] without Floor Drops or [[Hunting Wisdom]] being factored in.
[[Charm Chance]] does not affect the Critter Safari.
### Sparkling Critters
SPARKLING [[Critters]] have a 1/4,096 chance to replace any Critter in the Critter Safari. SPARKLING Critters are telegraphed with orange particles and a SPARKLING tag in their name, alongside wind chimes playing when nearby.
SPARKLING Critters drop 10x of their regular drops, alongside a [[Rainbow Feather]], with a 25% chance of dropping another with the [[Attributes|Splendor]] attribute. Additionally, SPARKLING Critters also have a 100x chance to drop an [[Emerald Dye]].
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Capturing a SPARKLING Critter|------------------------------------------- SPARKLING! <player> caught a SPARKLING <critter>! ------------------------------------------- CAPTURE! You caught a SPARKLING <critter> and received a Rainbow Feather and #x <shard>!|
|Lootsharing ([[Loot Sharing]]) a SPARKLING Critter|------------------------------------------- SPARKLING! <player> caught a SPARKLING <critter>! ------------------------------------------- LOOT SHARE! You received a Rainbow Feather and #x <shard> from <player> catching a SPARKLING <critter>!|
Catching SPARKLING Critters contributes to the [[Critterdex|Sparkling Critterdex]], which is unlocked after completing the [[Critterdex]] and grants cosmetic rewards as an optional grind. The [[Critterdex|Sparkling Critterdex]] does not grant [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. Unlike the normal [[Critterdex]], captures will unlock the [[Critterdex|Sparkling Critterdex]] entry for all players in the lobby.
The chances of spawning SPARKLING Critters can be increased by 2x with a [[Sparkling Amulet]]: the player will spawn with one equipped if they have the Sparkling Specialist perk active, which can be purchased from [[Archie]] for 10,000 (156 stacks plus 16) [[Safari Essence]]. The bonus of the [[Sparkling Amulet]] does not stack.

## Quest Items
Quest Items are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items found around the Critter Safari that are used to interact with the environment, usually leading to exclusive [[Attributes|Shards]] that the player otherwise would not be able to access.
### Floor Drops
A limited number of Floor Drops will appear throughout the different biomes in the Critter Safari: they do not respawn. Floor Drops may contain Quest Items, [[Safari Essence]], [[Hunting|Hunting XP]], or a [[Attributes|Shard]].
For more information on each Quest Item, view their respective articles.
|Cavern|Forest|Haunted|Icy|
|-|-|-|-|
[1–8 Safari Essence]
[2,500-10,000 Hunting XP (Hunting)]
|1 [[Flitter Shard]]|1 [[Foxtrot Shard]]|1 [[Litterbug Shard]]|1 [[Strongarm Shard]]|
|1 [[Shyworm Shard]]|1 [[Honeybug Shard]]|1 [[Solsnatcher Shard]]|1 [[Polaris Shard]]|
|1 [[Purple Gem]]|1 [[Yogi Berry]]|1 [[Soothing Incense]]|1 [[Icebreaker]]|
|1 [[Lime Gem]]|1 [[Wriggleworm]]|1 [[Shining Coin]]||
|1 [[Orange Gem]]|1 [[Bag of Seeds]]|||
### Harvestable Resources
Players can harvest [[Wholesale Wheat]], punch fish to get [[Flavor-packed Fish]], and break lily pads to get [[Lush Lily Pad|Lush Lily Pads]]. These resources can be found and used in the Cavern biome.
### Birdfeeder
The Birdfeeder is found in the Forest Biome, and is used to spawn the three types of birds.
|Food Used|Buffed Bird; (1.5x weight)|Birdfeeder Chances|
|Food Used|Buffed Bird; (1.5x weight)|[[Bluebird]] Chance|[[Parakeet]] Chance|[[Macaw]] Chance|
|[[Yogi Berry]]|[[Bluebird]]|72% chance (18 weight)|24% chance (6 weight)|4% chance (1 weight)|
|[[Wriggleworm]]|[[Parakeet]]|54.54% chance (12 weight)|40.91% chance (9 weight)|4.54% chance (1 weight)|
|[[Bag of Seeds]]|[[Macaw]]|61.54% chance (12 weight)|30.77% chance (6 weight)|7.69% chance (1.5 weight)|
## Safari Belt
The [[Safari Belt]] is the only item outside the Critter Safari the player may bring with them. It increments in rarity and stats depending on their Safari Milestones, which can be viewed at [[Andrea]].
- Successful hunts in the Cavern biome grant [[Hunting Wisdom]] and increases its rarity.
- Successful hunts in the Forest biome grant [[Foraging Fortune]].
- Successful hunts in the Haunted biome grant [[Hunting Fortune]].
- Successful hunts in the Icy biome grant [[Cold Resistance]].

The player can also salvage [[Attributes|Shards]] from the Critter Safari for [[Safari Essence]]. Conversion rates are as follows:
- [[Rarity|COMMON]] → 1 [[Safari Essence]]
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] → 2 [[Safari Essence]]
- [[Rarity|RARE]] → 4 [[Safari Essence]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] → 8 [[Safari Essence]]
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] → 16 [[Safari Essence]]
### Hunting Hotspot
If the player has the Hunting Hotspot perk unlocked at [[Archie]], a random biome will be selected as the player's Hunting Hotspot. While in a Hunting Hotspot, the player gains +5-25 [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Bells
There are 7 Bells scattered around the Critter Safari at different locations. Upon finding all 7 Bells and returning to [["Hunter" Tobias]], a cutscene begins to play. For the sake of spoilers, the following section will be hidden.
Show/Hide Bell Coordinates
- [[Coordinates|-68, 66, -43]]: A Bell is hidden on the bottom of an ice platform near the entrance to the Icy biome. Jumping on the platform repeatedly will cause it to flip over, yielding the [[Achievements|What the flip?!]] secret achievement. The bell will be too cold to ring, requiring an [[Icebreaker]] to break it.
- [[Coordinates|-96, 46, -57]]: A Bell is hidden behind a bed found in an underground cavern in the underwater lake in the Icy biome.
- [[Coordinates|-4, 96, -42]]: A Bell is hidden on the roof of the mansion in the Haunted biome. It can be accessed from parkour from the bottom of the mansion, or from climbing to the top of the Icy biome and jumping into the roof.
- [[Coordinates|47, 55, -7]]: A Bell is hidden at the edge of the Critter Safari, accessed from the Forest biome waterfalls, before dropping down and walking towards the Haunted biome while on the outer rim of the Critter Safari. The ledge can be exited safely by ascending from the vine at [[Coordinates|16, 53, 79]].
- [[Coordinates|-30, 125, 59]]: A Bell is hidden near the top of the Forest biome, requiring parkour across a large tree to a floating island.
- [[Coordinates|-90, 109, 16]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the Cavern biome: you will need to climb from a fake cactus onto a large stack from [[Coordinates|-138, 102, 26]], parkour across a tree, and make it to another stack where the bell is found.
- [[Coordinates|-50, 81, 0]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the landing zone, accessible from an ice spike by the entrance to the Icy biome.
Each bell corresponds to a specific color in the rainbow. Completing the rainbow and returning to the landing zone yields the following dialogue from [["Hunter" Tobias]]:
[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Uh guys, you better come and see this...
Finally, a cutscene plays as the player walks into the landing zone. As the player looks at the rainbow, the following dialogue plays:
Wow, a rainbow. I haven't seen one of them in a long time. Isn't it beautiful? You know, there are so many memories in a rainbow. I remember seeing one while playing with all my friends, back when we were very small. The next time I saw one was many years later, after one of them died. Sometimes it feels like, every time I see one, there are less of my kind left to see it with me. But it's still oddly comforting. Before I was here, there were rainbows. After I am gone, there will still be rainbows. Throughout my short life, everything I once knew has changed beyond recognition. As for the rainbows, I don't think they notice the difference.
With each dialogue, the player's [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] attribute gains +1 level, maxing out upon the cutscene ending. Players also receive [[Achievements|The Miracle in a sunbeam]] secret achievement.
During this cutscene, a custom [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|song]] plays:

## Tips
- Never enter the Critter Safari solo. Almost everything except for regular [[Critterdex]] progress and [[Emerald Dye|Emerald Dyes]] is shared. Additionally, more players allow for a faster run. It is usually recommended to have at least 3 players, preferably 4.
- In the [[Essence Shops|Safari Essence Shop]], Morning Dew and Critter Master are one of the most important upgrades to obtain.
  - The [[Morning Dew]] grants the [[Safari Belt]] 50 [[Speed]] while in the Critter Safari, allowing for faster clears, along a handful of stats that are useful within it. It is also currently the only [[Equipment]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] that grants [[Hunting]] stats.
  - The [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] has a 100% capture rate. It is best used on the [[Doomspiral]], [[Wumpa]], and, if it spawns, [[Macaw]], which would allow up to 20 capsules to be saved.
    - Players may also wish to use it on a [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]] or SPARKLING critter if they do not need to capture any of the above [[Critters|Critter]] (such as if they are clearing the Cavern biome) or are running low on capsules.
- There are 2 types of Critter Safari runs: Full Clear and Unique runs.
  - In a Full Clear run, players aim to capture all critters for shards. To effectively do this, it is a good strategy to let each player clear one biome. Hotspots could be used as a reference for allocations.
    - Alternatively, two players could overlap the Cavern biome, one for the upper part and one for the lower, to achieve a faster run. This is because this biome takes the longest to clear by far.
    - [[Masterful Critter Capsule|Masterful Critter Capsules]] are best to used on the [[Doomspiral]], the [[Wumpa]], and the two [[Macaw|Macaws]] if they spawn.
  - In a Unique run, players trade off the amount of shards obtained for the speed of capturing all unique critters. Such runs are the quickest way for grinding [[Hunting]] experience by far.
    - For mobs that are difficult to catch, such as [[Treefrog|Treefrogs]], their duplicate captures are usually discarded for efficiency.
    - The common strategy is to allocate each player to a biome. As the amount of shards is no longer the goal, Hotspots are usually ignored. There would be no need to overlap the Cavern biome.
    - Players may wish to use a [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] on a [[Gemzie]].
- SPARKLING critters that do not use vanilla textures (such as [[Shyworm]]) have custom textures when they spawn that differ from their normal texture. This can be used to determine if critters such as [[Troodon]] and [[Hideyho]] are SPARKLING before interacting with them, as they do not have name tags before being interacted with.
- Usually it is better to spend a bit of downtime between runs to get [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] on [[Starlyn Contest|Miria's Contest]] whenever there is a new contest to secure more safari tickets for more runs.
- Always use a First-Class ticket unless you are hunting only for SPARKLING critters. The additional [[Safari Essence]] will most likely outweigh the cost in upgrading tickets, and the additional [[Hunting Wisdom]] is also nice to have.
## Notes
- [[Bestiary]] progress, [[Safari Belt]] Milestones progress, [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] drops and [[Critterdex|Sparkling Critterdex]] entries are shared with everyone in a party — effectively everything except loot obtained from [[Floor Drops]], trading with NPCs and unlocking entries for the regular [[Critterdex]].
  - While Attribute Shard drops are shared, every player's [[Hunting Fortune]] is counted for them individually.
- The [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] gain from Critter Safari is affected by [[Hunting Wisdom]] that the player has inside Safari. It is also affected by [[Derpy]]'s MOAR SKILLZ!!! perk.
- Unlike with the [[Catacombs]], players who leave the Critter Safari cannot reenter.
## Trivia
- The release of the Critter Safari marks the first time since [[SkyBlock]]'s release where more than one island has been released in one update.
- Excluding [[Gimmiegold|Gimmiegolds]], there are on average 40 critters in the Cavern biome, 33.91 critters in the Forest biome, 33 critters in the Haunted biome and 35 critters in the Icy biome, for a total of 141.91 critters.
  - The chance of getting at least one SPARKLING critter is then approximately 3.4%, raised to 6.6% with a [[Sparkling Amulet]].
- A Critter Safari instance will automatically close after around 60 minutes regardless of progress or player activity.

# Critter Safari Entrance
Aliases: Safari Zone Entrance
Categories: Locations
[Critter Safari Entrance]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance]]
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]); [[Heart of the Forest]] Tier 4
Music: "Torrhus Canyon ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -720 99 183
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Andrea]] [[Archie]] [[Safari Manager]] [[Safari Receptionist]]
The Critter Safari Entrance is a [[Locations|Location]] found on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It serves as the entrance to the [[Critter Safari]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Critter Safari Entrance, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Critter Safari Entrance
■ Talk to the Safari Manager to enter the Critter Safari.
■ Talk to Andrea to view your Safari Milestones.
■ Talk to Archie to view the Safari Essence Shop.
## Features
The Critter Safari Entrance serves as the introduction and entry point to the [[Critter Safari]]. Players can access the Safari by speaking to the [[Safari Manager]] and using [[Safari Tickets]], which can be obtained from [[Miria]]'s [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]].
The [[Safari Receptionist]] gives players information about the Safari. Upstairs, [[Andrea]] gives players the [[Safari Belt]], which can be upgraded through [[Andrea|Safari Milestones]], and [[Archie]] hosts the [[Safari Essence]] Shop.
## NPCs
There are 4 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Critter Safari Entrance:

|[[Archie]]|[[Archie]]|Critter Safari Entrance; -706.5, 108, 165.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells perks and upgrades in exchange for [[Safari Essence]].|✔|
|[[Safari Manager]]|[[Safari Manager]]|Critter Safari Entrance; -732.5, 99.0625, 185.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access the [[Critter Safari]].|✕|
|[[Safari Receptionist]]|[[Safari Receptionist]]|Critter Safari Entrance; -719.5, 99.0625, 178.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the [[Critter Safari]].|✕|

The Crypts are a [[Locations|Location]] within the [[Hub Island]], accessible through the [[Graveyard]] or a [[Cracked Wall|weak wall]] in the [[Coal Mine]]. It contains [[Crypt Ghoul|Crypt Ghouls]] and [[Golden Ghoul|Golden Ghouls]] and primarily serves as a place to complete [[Zombie Slayer]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Crypts, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Crypts
■ Explore the Crypts.
■ Watch out for the Zombies that lurk here!

The Crystal Hollows is a location that is focused on [[Mining]]. The primary focuses of the Crystal Hollows are mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] and collecting [[Gemstone Crystals]] to complete the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Crystal Hollows, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Crystal Hollows
■ Mine to your heart's content!
■ Mine new ores and fight new enemies!
■ Discover Crystals to earn rewards and forge Perfect Gems!
## Overview
The Crystal Hollows consists of a large series of tunnels made of [[Hard Stone]], with veins of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] and randomly-generated structures scattered throughout the caves. The tunnels themselves remain the same across lobbies, but the locations of structures will always be different across different servers. At the center of the Crystal Hollows houses the [[Crystal Nucleus]], where [[Gemstone Crystals]] can be placed.
Talking to [[Gwendolyn]] allows you to purchase a pass to the Crystal Hollows for 6h for 10,000 [[Coins]]: you gain permanent, unrestricted access to the Crystal Hollows after helping [[Dulin]].
### Mining
While inside the Crystal Hollows, broken ores and blocks are not replaced with bedrock. Instead, they will physically be broken and replaced with air, similar to vanilla Minecraft. Blocks can be broken everywhere except the [[Crystal Nucleus]] and in specific structures: [[Torch|Torches]], [[Ladder|Ladders]], [[Hard Stone]], and [[Cobblestone]], alongside [[Granite]], [[Andesite]], and their Polished Variants can also be placed.
[[Stone]] is replaced by [[Hard Stone]] inside the Crystal Hollows, which has a higher [[Breaking Power]] and a unique Collection. [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], [[Mithril]], and most Ores can spawn inside the Crystal Hollows. Ores that require a Furnace to be smelted into an ingot, such as [[Ores|Iron Ore]] and [[Ores|Gold Ore]], do not require [[Smelting Touch]] or a Furnace to be converted to an ingot.
Yields from most vanilla ores are increased within the Crystal Hollows.
Ore Yields:
|Icon|Ore|Base Yield|
|-|-|-|
|[[Ores]]|Coal Ore|4–6 [[Coal]]|
|[[Ores]]|Lapis Lazuli Ore|9–14 [[Lapis Lazuli]]|
|[[Ores]]|Redstone Ore|9–14 [[Redstone Dust]]|
|[[Ores]]|Emerald Ore|4–6 [[Emerald]]|
|[[Ores]]|Diamond Ore|4–6 [[Diamond]]|
## Zones
There are 5 main locations in the Crystal Hollows, being the [[Jungle]], [[Mithril Deposits]], [[Precursor Remnants]], [[Goblin Holdout]], and [[Magma Fields]]. These correspond to their respective [[Gemstone]], that being [[Gemstone|Amethyst]], [[Gemstone|Jade]], [[Gemstone|Sapphire]], [[Gemstone|Amber]], and [[Gemstone|Topaz]]. [[Gemstone|Ruby]] can also be found in the Crystal Hollows and can be found everywhere. Additionally, 3 [[Fairy Grotto|Fairy Grottos]] spawn in random locations throughout the Crystal Hollows: [[Gemstone|Jasper]] can be mined there.
Each primary location has a sub-location, where its respective [[Gemstone Crystals|Gemstone Crystal]] can be obtained.
- The [[Jungle]] contains the [[Jungle Temple]], where a [[Jungle Key]] must be given to access a series of parkour with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] at the end
- The [[Mithril Deposits]] contains the [[Mines of Divan]], where a [[Metal Detector]] must be used to find and return [[Scavenged Items]], with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] being rewarded at the end
- The [[Precursor Remnants]] contains the [[Lost Precursor City]], where all 6 [[Professor Robot|Automaton Parts]] or a [[Precursor Apparatus]] must be given to access the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]]
- The [[Goblin Holdout]] contains the [[Goblin Queen's Den]], where any variant of [[Goblin Egg]] must be given to [[King Yolkar]] the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]] without being caught
- The [[Magma Fields]] contains [[Khazad-dûm]], where [[Bal]] must be defeated to claim the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]].
Gemstone Crystals can be placed in the [[Crystal Nucleus]] or used to forge Perfect Gemstones. You can see the status of Gemstone Crystals in the tablist: a Crystal can either be Not Found, Not Placed, or Placed. A Gemstone Crystal must be found but Not Placed to be used in the Forge to forge its respective Perfect Gemstone.

## Treasure Chests
Treasure Chests exist in 2 types : Dug Chests and Structure Chests. They are found exclusively in the Crystal Hollows. They are the main way of gaining ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]].
## Metal Detector Chests
Metal Detector Chests are exclusively found among the [[Gold Ingot|Pure Gold]] coating the floor of the [[Mines of Divan]] using a [[Metal Detector]]. They are relevant in obtaining the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]].
## Structures
Each part of the Crystal Hollows has its own main special location, excluding [[Magma Fields]], which will almost always generate in every lobby:
- The [[Jungle Temple]] generated in the [[Jungle]]
- The [[Mines of Divan]] generated in the [[Mithril Deposits]]
- The [[Goblin Queen's Den]] generated in the [[Goblin Holdout]]
- The [[Lost Precursor City]] generated in the [[Precursor Remnants]]
- 3 [[Fairy Grotto|Fairy Grottos]] generated anywhere within the Crystal Hollows
Each of these structures house their respective [[Crystal Nucleus|Crystal]]. A special task must be completed to obtain the Crystal.
[[Khazad-dûm]] is the one location that can spawn in any part of the Crystal Hollows, making it harder to find.
There are also smaller structures which generate in random locations. Examples include:
- A structure which resembles the [[Deep Caverns]], including the lift and the layered structure of the original Deep Caverns island
- A dragon skull from the [[Spider's Den]]
- A replica of the [[Dark Auction]]
- A miniature version of [[The Forge]] including a working forge NPC
- A copy of the pre-[[Hub Island|Hub]] overhaul [[Tavern]]
A full list of Structures can be found at [[Crystal Hollows/Special Locations]].
Certain structures contain loot chests. Loot chests always generate in the same positions within the structures, similar to [[Dungeon Secrets]]. Loot Chests also generate the same items for every player, meaning if a player finds a chest with good loot such as a lot of Gemstone Powder or a Blue Goblin Egg, they can send the coordinates to chat to lead others to the chest.

## Trivia
- The life of a Crystal Hollows lobby is determined by the following rules:
  - On Day 0 (1st day), the lobby opens to players;
  - On Day 19 (20th day), the lobby becomes locked, preventing new players from entering. Henceforth, if there are less than 4 players, then the lobby will start shutting down.
  - On Day 35 (36th day), the lobby will forcefully start shutting down regardless of player count.
- The Crystal Hollows are internally referred to as the Greater Mines in the SkyBlock API.
- The Crystal Hollows are the only place [[Dirt]], [[Andesite]], [[Diorite]], and [[Granite]] (and other blocks) can be mined, however, only [[Dirt]] drops itself and all others drop [[Hard Stone]] instead.
- [[Ores]] within a few blocks of the [[Bedrock]] border may not get auto-smelted without [[Smelting Touch]].
- Attempting to get into the Crystal Hollows using an [[Ender Pearl]] will teleport the player out, even if they have a valid Crystal Hollows Pass. See Game Messages.

The Crystal Nucleus is a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]], mainly used to deposit collected [[Gemstone Crystals]] from expeditions.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Crystal Nucleus, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Crystal Nucleus
■ Place all Crystals at the statues.
## Location
The Crystal Nucleus is always in the middle of the [[Crystal Hollows]] [[Coordinates|512, Y, 512]]. Players can travel to main areas of the map using each of 4 quadrants, as well as the [[Magma Fields]] directly below the platform. After the required Crystals ([[Gemstone Crystals|Jade]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire]], and [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz]]) are delivered to their respective statues, a Crystal Nucleus Loot Bundle will appear in front of the vault opposite of the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]] Statue.
Crystals will remain placed if you leave the game. They cannot be picked up once placed, nor can duplicate Crystals be obtained until all 5 Crystals are placed.
## Chat Messages
|Condition|Associated Text|
|-|-|
|Placing a Crystal|✦ You placed the <type> Crystal! Keep exploring the Crystal Hollows to find the rest!|
|After placing all Crystals|You've earned a Crystal Loot Bundle!|
Note: The color of "<type> Crystal" changes based on the Crystal type
## Loot
- Each Crystal Nucleus Bundle contains :
  - 14-19 pieces of random loot;
  - 1 of every Fine [[Gemstone]] found in the [[Crystal Hollows]] (e.g., not including Opal, Onyx, Aquamarine, Citrine, and Peridot);
  - 800 [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM EXP]]. If the player is already [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain 10]], this is replaced with a random amount between 2,000-7,000 of either [[Mithril Powder]] or [[Gemstone Powder]].
The number of drops can be increased in the following ways, up to a range of 16-21 rolls with a 65% chance for a bonus roll:
- by 1, using [[Mole Pet]]
- by 1, using [[Essence Shops|Diamond Essence Shop perk "High Roller"]];
- by 1 at a 5%→50% chance, using the [[Attributes|Bigger Box]] attribute ( [[Hideoncave Shard]])
  - which can be boosted up to 1.20x, using the [[Attributes|Echo of Boxes]] attribute ( [[Cuboa Shard]]), resulting in a 6%→60% chance from the [[Attributes|Bigger Box]] attribute ( [[Hideoncave Shard]])
    - which can be boosted up to 1.50x, using the [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] attribute ( [[Tiamat Shard]]); resulting in a maximum multipler of 1.30x from the [[Attributes|Bigger Box]] attribute ( [[Hideoncave Shard]]); resulting in a maximum chance range 6.5%→65% chance from the [[Attributes|Bigger Box]] attribute ( [[Hideoncave Shard]]).
Using [[Bazaar|Bazaar Prices]]:
- The cost of 1 [[Precursor Apparatus]], 1 [[Jungle Key]], and 1 [[Goblin Egg]], minus the sell price of the guaranteed [[Gemstone|Fine Gemstones]] drops, means each run costs .
- The cost of 1 of each [[Professor Robot|Automaton Part]], 1 [[Jungle Key]], and 1 [[Goblin Egg]], minus the sell price of the guaranteed [[Gemstone|Fine Gemstones]] drops, means each run costs .

There is also a 0.0002% (1 in 500,000 rolls) chance for each roll to be replaced with a [[Jade Dye]].
## RNG Meter
The Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter increases the drop rate of certain items from the list below.
- Placing all crystals at the Crystal Nucleus gains 1,000 Nucleus XP for the meter. Retroactive completions from before the release of the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter grant 250 Nucleus XP rounded towards the nearest thousand.
- Items in the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter have predefined amounts of XP required to guarantee to roll.
- The amount of XP is roughly based on drop chance. The XP values can be read in the list below.
- When a bundle is rolled, the selected item has an increased weight with the following formula. If enough XP is stored within the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter, the selected item is guaranteed to drop.
  - New Weight=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Crystal Nucleus XPRequired Crystal Nucleus XP,2))
- The menu can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]]: [[SkyBlock Menu]] ——> [[Skills|Your Skills]] ——> [[Mining]] ——> [[Heart of the Mountain]] ——> [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]
Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter XP Values:

## Trivia
- Successfully rolling for any [[Gemstone|Flawless Gemstone]] from a Nucleus Loot Bundle is much more common than [[Divan's Alloy]], by combining their weights:
  Item: 1 Any Flawless Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Weight: 95, Chance per Roll: 0.030923% (1 in 3,233.8 rolls), Chance per Bundle (16.5 rolls): 0.510234% (1 in 196.0 bundles), Chance per Bundle (19.15 rolls): 0.592181% (1 in 168.9 bundles); Item: 1 [[Divan's Alloy]], Weight: 17, Chance per Roll: 0.005534% (1 in 18,071.3 rolls), Chance per Bundle (16.5 rolls): 0.091305% (1 in 1,095.2 bundles), Chance per Bundle (19.15 rolls): 0.105969% (1 in 943.7 bundles)
- Contrary to popular belief, the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]'s formula is indeed applied to all rolls.
- The [[Gemstone Crystals|Jasper]] and [[Gemstone Crystals|Ruby]] Crystals are not used to complete the Crystal Nucleus but may be used to forge their respective [[The Forge|Perfect Gemstones]].
- The Crystal Nucleus is the only location in the [[Crystal Hollows]] that generates at a set location.
- The [[Gemstone|Fine Gemstones]] guaranteed at each crystal run ensure at least 179,200 [[Coins]] (by selling to NPCs) or (by selling to the Bazaar).
- The center is positioned at [[Coordinates|513, Y, 513]] and roughly encloses a space from [[Coordinates|465, 64, 465]] to [[Coordinates|561, 188, 561]], meaning it is not centered in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
- Crystals used to be reclaimable after placing in the Crystal Nucleus. If no item was picked up from the Loot Bundle, players could swap lobbies and replace the last crystal, rolling for new rewards. This was known as Nucleus Rerolling.
- It's possible to enter the Nucleus Vault by mining in through the top or from behind.

# Dark Auction
Aliases: DA, Dark Auction House, Darker Auction, Darker Auctions
Categories: Locations, Article stubs, Dark Auction, Events, Hidden Locations
Dark Auction
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
[Dark Auction]
Type: Market
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Dark Auction]]
Level: No level prerequisites, but player must be holding 400,000 [[Coins]] on entry
Location: [[Wilderness]]
Coordinates: 91 75 176
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Bob]] [[Scorpius]] [[Sirius]]
Are you here for the Auction? Only the richest players can enter.
— [[Sirius]]
The Dark Auction is an event which occurs every 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]], or every real-life hour. During this event, exclusive items are sold to the highest bidder.
## Mechanics
The Dark Auction occurs every 3 SkyBlock days at midnight, usually occurring at the 55-minute mark of every hour. [[Sirius]] will appear: players with a minimum of 400,000 [[Coins]] in the purse will be eligible to enter. Only the richest players will be allowed to enter the Dark Auction.
Items in the Dark Auction will be presented for 5s before players can place bids on them, after which players will have 10s to place their bids: however, the timer is reset every time a bid is placed. Players can only place predetermined amounts of [[Coins]]: all non-winning bids are refunded. Players who place the highest bid but disconnect before claiming it will have the coins automatically deducted and the item sent to [[Jerry]]'s [[Lost & Found]]. 5 items appear every Dark Auction, with the number increasing to 8 while [[Scorpius]] is mayor.
If no one places any bids on an item, the auction will end with no winner and this message will appear:
[NPC] Sirius: No one made any bids! Let's move on to the next item.
If it was the last item, the message will instead say:
[NPC] Sirius: No one made any bids!
## Auctioned Items
Items can only appear in certain positions of the Dark Auction's order. The auctioned items will be randomly selected from the possible items available from that round.
Each item may only appear once: items that appear cannot appear in any following rounds.

## Trivia
The [[Personal Bank]], [[Bazaar]], and [[Auction House]] cannot be accessed while inside the Dark Auction.
- Once the player has been in the Dark Auction once, the days of which the Dark Auction takes place will appear on their [[Calendar]]. The player will also receive 1,000 [[Coins]].
- Participating in a full Dark Auction for the first time will award the [[Achievements|Sirius Business]] achievement.
- The music played in the Dark Auction is C418 - Stal.
- During the [[Resistance Movement]] event, the Dark Auction functioned as a base for [[Resistance Movement|The Resistance]]. Here, players could elect on the [[Resistance Leader]] and [[Resistance General|Resistance Generals]].

# Dark Thicket
Categories: Locations
[Dark Thicket]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Sub-locations: [[Howling Cave]] [[Trials of Fire]]
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Park]]
Requirements: A Helping Hand ([[Quests]]) quest
← Previous Location: [[Spruce Woods]]
Next Location →: [[Savanna Woodland]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Campfire Adept]] [[Campfire Initiate]] [[Old Shaman Nyko]] [[Ryan]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Dark Oak Log]]
The Dark Thicket is the third section of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dark Thicket, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dark Thicket
■ Chop down Dark Oak logs.
■ Talk to Ryan about the Trial of Fire.
## Features
The Dark Thicket features a few attractions.
### Trials of Fire
The player can enter the campfire located next to [[Ryan]] to begin the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]]. They must survive 10s in the fire whilst it constantly deals [[True Damage]]. There are 30 Trials, each dealing more and more damage per second. After successfully completing a Trial, the player can talk to [[Ryan]] to upgrade their [[Campfire Badge]].
After completing the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]] for the first time, [[Ryan]] will ask the player for 256 (4 stacks) [[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak Logs]]. Giving these will complete "The Campfire Cult" [[Quests|Quest]], rewarding the player with the [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Initiate Badge]], 2,000 [[Coins]], +1,000 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], and access to the [[Savanna Woodland]].
### Howling Cave
Near the middle of the Dark Thicket lies a breakable wall that can be blown up with an [[Explosive Bow]] or [[Superboom TNT]]. Here, you will find the [[Howling Cave]], where [[Pack Spirit|Pack Spirits]], [[Howling Spirit|Howling Spirits]], and [[Soul of the Alpha|Soul of the Alphas]] will spawn. This is one of three caves designed for [[Wolf Slayer]]. [[Old Shaman Nyko]] resides here.

|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Trials of Fire]]; -358.5, 102.5, -94.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Trials of Fire]] and the campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Trials of Fire]]; -362.5, 102.5, -97.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Trials of Fire]] and the campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Old Shaman Nyko]]|[[Old Shaman Nyko]]|[[Howling Cave]]; -370.5, 84, -64.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells True Protection I ([[True Protection]]) and [[True Essence]].|✔|
|[[Ryan]]|[[Ryan]]|[[Trials of Fire]]; -364.5, 102.5, -90.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the [[Trials of Fire]].|✕|

# Deep Caverns
Aliases: Deep Cavern
Categories: Locations, Mining, Deep Caverns
[Deep Caverns]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns]]
Level: [[Mining]] level V
Sub-locations: [[Gunpowder Mines]] [[Lapis Quarry]] [[Pigmen's Den]] [[Slimehill]] [[Diamond Reserve]] [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]
Fairy Souls: 21
← Previous Location: [[Gold Mine]]
Next Location →: [[Gunpowder Mines]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Kuudra Loremaster]] [[Lapis Miner]] [[Lift Operator]] [[Redstone Miner]] [[Rhys]] [[Walter]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Emerald Slime]] [[Lapis Zombie]] [[Miner Skeleton]] [[Miner Zombie]] [[Redstone Pigman]] [[Sneaky Creeper]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] [[Exp Share Core]] [[Flaming Sword]] [[Gold Nugget]] [[Gunpowder]] Lapis Armor Piece ([[Lapis Armor]]) [[Lapis Crystal]] Miner Armor Piece ([[Miner Armor]]) [[Redstone Dust]] [[Rotten Flesh]] [[Slimeball]]
Resources: [[Coal]] [[Diamond]] [[Emerald]] Gold Ore ([[Ores]]) Iron Ore ([[Ores]]) [[Lapis Lazuli]] [[Obsidian]] [[Rare Diamond]] Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) [[Stone]]
The Deep Caverns is a location focused on [[Mining]]. The players requires [[Mining|Mining V (5)]] to enter. Several unique resources can be found: [[Lapis Lazuli]], [[Redstone Dust]], [[Emerald|Emeralds]], [[Diamond|Diamonds]], and [[Obsidian]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Deep Caverns, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Deep Caverns
■ Talk to the Lift Operator.
■ Mine valuable ores.
■ Watch out for mobs!
## Purpose
The Deep Caverns are divided into 6 layers (excluding the [[Dwarven Mines]]) that become progressively more dangerous the deeper traveled.
After discovering a layer for the first time, the player can be quickly travel to them with the [[Lift Operator]].
Each layer has its own resources and its own hostile mobs that increase in difficulty as the player progresses deeper into the caverns.
## Zones
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|

|^ [[Lapis Quarry]]|Primarily contains Lapis Lazuli Ore ([[Ores]]) for [[Lapis Lazuli]], the [[Lapis Miner]], and Lapis Zombies ([[Lapis Zombie]]).|[[Lapis Miner]], [[Lift Operator]]|
|^ [[Pigmen's Den]]|Primarily contains Gold Ore ([[Ores]]) for Gold Ingots ([[Gold Ingot]]), Redstone Pigmen ([[Redstone Pigman]]), and the [[Redstone Miner]].|[[Lift Operator]], [[Redstone Miner]]|
|^ [[Slimehill]]|Primarily contains Emerald Ore ([[Ores]]) for Emeralds ([[Emerald]]), as well as Slimes ([[Slime (disambiguation)]]).|[[Lift Operator]]|
|^ [[Diamond Reserve]]|Primarily contains Diamond Ore ([[Ores]]) for Diamonds ([[Diamond]]), as well as Miner Zombies ([[Miner Zombie]]) and Miner Skeletons ([[Miner Skeleton]]).|[[Lift Operator]]|
|^ [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]|Primarily contains Diamond Ore ([[Ores]]), Blocks of Diamond ([[Block of Diamond]]), and [[Obsidian]] Also contains Miner Zombies ([[Miner Zombie]]) and Miner Skeletons ([[Miner Skeleton]]), as well as [[Rhys]], who unlocks access to the [[Dwarven Mines]].|[[Lift Operator]], [[Rhys]]|

## Trivia
- The transition sections between levels does not spawn mobs from either floor and prevents them from entering said sections.
  - This can be exploited, as the transition between the [[Diamond Reserve]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] allows players to easily and safely mine high quantities of [[Diamond|Diamonds]].

# Desert Mountain

[Desert Mountain]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 2
Location: [[Mushroom Desert]]

Desert Mountain is an area in the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Desert Mountain, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Desert Mountain
■ Find Jake.
## Purpose
Desert Mountain is a collection of mountains on the [[Mushroom Desert]] that allows players to collect [[Cactus]] and [[Sand]]. [[Trevor|Trapper Animals]] can also spawn in this location. The top of the mountain contains [[Jake]].

The Desert Settlement is a sub-location of the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Desert Settlement, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Desert Settlement
■ Talk to the settlement inhabitants.
## Features
The Desert Settlement is located at the spawn of the [[Mushroom Desert]] and has 4 houses, 3 of which are inhabited, each by [[Beth]], [[Mason]] and the [[Friendly Hiker]].

## Trivia
- While [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] was [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] after [[Dante]]'s defeat, a [[Piggy|Technoshop]] appeared near the Desert Settlement for a limited time. It sold the same items as that in the [[Hub Island|Hub]]'s [[Community Center]].
- The empty house was most likely inhabited by the [[Hungry Hiker]] before he got lost in the [[Mushroom Gorge]].
